# v2026.9.5

You can now install or reload supported plugins without restarting the Gateway. This release also adds guided setup for teams of specialist agents and brings GPT Live to meetings and phone calls.

**Release scale:** 2,091 pull requests, 14 direct commits, and 273 contributors.

## Installation and Onboarding

Setup gives you more ways to shape a new agent, with clearer guidance when installation or a first connection needs attention.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Set up a specialist team">

Start with a chief of staff, researcher, writer and reviewer, or choose a single specialist. [Guided setup](/start/wizard) can create the four-agent team, and **New agent** in the web UI offers the same roles through a proposal you approve before anything is created. Setup remembers your chosen coordinator if you need to resume. Choosing **Skip** creates the team offline, with AI access still to configure.

If creation stops partway through, check `openclaw agents list` and repair the incomplete team before retrying. If all four members already exist, finish with `openclaw onboard --workspace <workspace>` without `--team`.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Offer specialist roles and approved team creation through New agent [#141453](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141453).
- Create role-based agents and coordinator-led teams with separate workspaces [#145712](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145712).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve the selected coordinator through provider setup and validate the complete team when resuming onboarding [#145817](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145817).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose an avatar for a new agent">

Once you agree on your new agent's name and personality, setup can offer four matching portraits to choose from. This needs an available image provider and saves your choice after you select it. You can keep the emoji by skipping, and setup can continue with it if generation or saving fails.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Offer four generated avatar choices during new-workspace setup while preserving existing avatars [#146669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146669).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Start with fewer setup decisions">

Fresh OpenAI and ChatGPT/Codex setups default to Astra when you have not chosen a primary model. Existing model choices stay in place. If your account does not offer Astra, choose an available model during [OpenAI setup](/providers/openai/setup). Verified official plugins also skip the redundant capability approval on the way to authentication, while third-party and unverifiable plugins still need the applicable [consent](/cli/plugins/install).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Simplify verified official plugin setup, use Astra for fresh OpenAI/Codex defaults and related image and Workboard fallbacks, and honor existing model policies [#145646](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145646).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover command-line installation">

The installer reuses compatible Node and nvm installations without unnecessary system replacement or shell-profile changes. If it needs to install Node through nvm, it asks first and explains any default-alias changes. Declining or running unattended leaves you with preparation commands.

Mac app CLI setup can now recover from temporary-directory permission errors without an administrator password, provided `/tmp` is writable.

On Linux, Gateway installation can recover from stale session connection settings while preserving working custom connections to the service manager. It also works again on systems running systemd 239. If the service manager is unavailable or its files cannot be read, the error explains what needs repair.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve useful error messages when automatic Control UI builds fail in source checkouts [#144844](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144844). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Preserve existing Node and nvm setups, request consent for a new runtime, and avoid npm settings that break nvm [#145317](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145317). Thanks @jb-lopez.
- Restore Gateway installation and reinstallation on Linux systems running systemd 239 [#146022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146022). Thanks @pollybot13 and @bruce-cham.
- Correct understated download-speed estimates during llama.cpp model and runtime setup [#146263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146263).
- Recover Mac CLI setup from temporary-directory permission failures and restore the original environment before service setup [#146730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146730).
- Recover Linux Gateway installation from stale session-bus settings, preserve custom connections during update checks, and show the selected connection in deep status [#146569](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146569).
- Preserve working runtime links when replacement validation fails and keep relative-path selections usable after changing directories. Alpine system-package changes are not rolled back [#147221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147221). Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.
- Respect intentional current-directory entries in custom PATH settings without introducing a lookup when all search locations are excluded [#147249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147249). Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.
- Preserve the last complete CLI startup and help metadata when a refresh fails during writing or replacement [#147290](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147290).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Set up OpenClaw in a container">

The [Docker guide](/install/docker#using-the-control-ui-browser) now explains how to set up the web UI's browser inside your container and troubleshoot missing Chromium. If an in-container package update fails because of permissions, OpenClaw points you toward replacing the image and redeploying with your existing state and configuration mounts. Docker source builds that select Matrix also avoid the import failures that could stop image preparation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Explain image redeployment after container package-update permission failures [#95887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/95887). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev and @dioubernardo.

**Bug fixes**

- Fix Docker source builds that select Matrix [#146081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146081). Thanks @vincentkoc.

**Documentation**

- Document Control UI browser setup in Docker, preserving existing deployment settings and troubleshooting mounted-home Chromium paths [#141527](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141527). Thanks @pollybot13 and @patrick-erichsen.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Connect a device with clearer guidance">

Device approval and rejection now tolerate accidental spaces or newlines around a pasted request ID. Authorized operators using trusted-proxy authentication can also generate [mobile setup codes](/cli/qr) without adding an unused Gateway token or password. Your phone must still authenticate through the proxy, and scanning a QR code does not sign you into Cloudflare Access. Secure-transport rules and pairing approval remain in place, and authentication mode `none` is still rejected. Simple local-network pairing skips an unnecessary route lookup when only one eligible address is available.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip unnecessary route probes during LAN pairing with one eligible private IPv4 address [#145812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145812).

**Bug fixes**

- Accept device pairing request IDs with surrounding whitespace in Gateway approval and rejection calls [#110848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/110848). Thanks @nocodet888-arch and @obviyus.
- Name the configured authentication mode when pairing-code generation fails while retaining working credential fallbacks [#144136](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144136). Thanks @liuwqgit, @obviyus and @delicate-xqy.
- Reject blank environments in `openclaw nodes push` before connecting. Omit `--environment` to use the node's default, or supply `sandbox` or `production` [#144687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144687). Thanks @alix-007 and @obviyus.
- Allow authorized setup-code generation for trusted-proxy Gateways without a shared token or password, while retaining proxy sign-in and pairing controls [#147036](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147036). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Wrap long Node host pairing commands inside the device dialog, keep the full command available to copy, and keep controls reachable by scrolling on mobile [#147427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147427). Thanks @donniefi and @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep your default agent after migration">

Migrated multi-agent setups now keep their saved default after a restart, so integrations can find the intended agent again. The web UI saves and displays that same choice. If your agent list has no valid default, choose one with **Set Default** in the web UI. Choosing a default does not move your older workspaces or conversation data.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve migrated default-agent discovery after restart, save the web UI's default choice correctly, and keep explicit targets and existing data ownership intact [#146246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146246). Thanks @lilei0311.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Install the CLI on FreeBSD">

FreeBSD users can now install the CLI with `install-cli.sh` using system Node and npm. Have an administrator install `bash`, `node24`, `npm-node24`, `git`, `python3` and `gmake` first. Those system packages remain administrator-managed, and the installer's private `--node-only` recovery option is unavailable on FreeBSD.

Setup and error messages explain how to connect to a Gateway you start externally, using the same account you used for onboarding. You can run `openclaw gateway run` in a terminal or manage an installed package service separately. The CLI does not install or control FreeBSD services, and its service status remains unknown.

On affected FreeBSD 2026.9.4 installations, the updater stops before it can download the process-identification fix. Install a release containing the fix through the package manager that owns your installation first.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Install the FreeBSD CLI with validated system Node and npm, preserving existing runtime links when prerequisites fail [#147204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147204). Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.
- Explain external Gateway service management and use `openclaw node run` for node-host startup on FreeBSD [#147200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147200). Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.

**Bug fixes**

- Correct FreeBSD desktop setup guidance to explain system prerequisites and connection to an externally started Gateway [#147211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147211). Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Read FreeBSD's stable kernel boot identity for update and triage ownership checks, supplying one prerequisite for broader update support [#147243](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147243). Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.
- Identify FreeBSD x64 and arm64 processes for update handoffs and file-lock ownership checks, adding another prerequisite for broader update support [#147415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147415). Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep migration options in effect">

Migration commands now keep your selected source, output format and credential exclusions when shared options appear before `list`, `plan` or `apply`. Options explicitly set on a subcommand still take precedence. Use [`openclaw migrate plan`](/cli/migrate) to preview a migration. The `apply` command does not support dry runs and now rejects a preceding `--dry-run` before changing anything.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Honor shared migration options before subcommands, retain a parent `--no-auth-credentials` when the subcommand explicitly includes secrets, and reject unsupported dry-run requests before applying a migration [#144111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144111). Thanks @yetval.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## The New Web UI

The web UI makes it easier to find an agent, follow its work, and keep a conversation going while the rest of the page catches up.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Find agents and their conversations">

The new Agents overview brings recent activity and conversation previews together, with a direct link to each agent’s main chat. Turn on **Show all agents** for an optional [team sidebar](/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar) that groups conversations by agent. That choice is remembered in this browser for each Gateway, and agent creation remains available in Settings.

Activity keeps delegated subagent work with its parent conversation, so it no longer crowds out your conversations or inflates people counts. Switching agents also cancels obsolete archive-deletion confirmations from the previous agent.

Choose whether empty sidebar groups hide **When filtering**, **Always**, or **Never**, with separate choices for each user and Gateway in this browser. To give a conversation its own artwork, paste a custom SVG in **Icon & color → Custom icon…**, or ask your agent to set it. Artwork keeps its own colors and must be self-contained and no larger than 16 KiB decoded. Scripts, embedded documents, and external references are rejected.

A Beam row now disappears while a confirmed deletion is pending and returns with an error if deletion fails. Successful deletion remains permanent. Independent continuations stay available, and a later upload can recreate the Beam row.

<Accordion title="Choose where a new task runs">

New Session puts your devices in a searchable picker, with capacity details and an **Any available device** choice when you have several. Cloud options sit beside their provider, and suggested tasks can start in the current conversation, a new session, or a separate [Git worktree](/concepts/managed-worktrees).

A worktree needs a Git checkout. **Local** means the computer running OpenClaw, which may be different from the computer running your browser.

<Accordion title="Keep drafts and queued input intact">

You can submit text and attachments while [chat history loads](/web/control-ui/chat), then start your next draft immediately. Delivery waits until the conversation is ready. During a reconnect, an attempted message appears once in the conversation, while messages that have not been attempted stay editable in the queue. You can also save a correction if the connection drops while you edit a waiting, never-attempted message. Attachment preparation now has a visible status, and removal controls identify the file by name.

**Discard** removes the local pending copy; it cannot cancel a message OpenClaw has already received. Most commands still need the conversation to finish loading. If saving a queued correction fails because of browser storage, keep the tab open and copy the affected text before freeing storage. For saved drafts that fail to load, reload without clearing site data, after copying any unsaved correction; clearing site data would delete local drafts.

<Accordion title="Read replies and long histories">

Loading earlier messages now keeps you on the message you were reading, including when you choose **Show earlier**. Completed answers stay visible after later tool activity, and Japanese, Chinese, and Korean bold text and multiline table cells display as intended. Desktop navigation groups loaded assistant responses by run, while long user messages have visible **Show more** and **Show less** controls.

Background-task notifications imported from Claude appear as collapsed system notices, with the original content available to expand. The same XML deliberately pasted by a person remains their message.

- Group desktop navigation marks by assistant run and preserve position through loaded continuations [d534c315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d534c3153d3460ebb1d7e2f2d7876617643db7de). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Show native Claude background-task notifications as collapsed system notices while keeping operator-pasted XML as user text [#145772](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145772). Thanks @marvinthebored, @peetiegonzalez, @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect tool activity when needed">

Tool failures take up less room in chat, with a muted count beside collapsed activity and full errors available when you expand it. Named operations now keep their child commands together, and plugin-supplied icons follow the current theme color to help you recognize tools. Background-task details use a compact, full-width feed with expandable commands, while assistant text keeps growing as one message through tool activity. A **Saving conversation memory…** status explains that part of a wait before a reply.

Web and macOS chat show each named subagent task with its status alongside it. In the web UI, task progress folds while you read older messages and opens on return to the latest messages, subject to your existing collapse preference. A manual choice takes priority through completion in that open view and resets for a new task, another session, or a recreated card.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show plugin icons on live and restored chat tool calls [#143711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143711). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Make background-task details readable with a compact activity feed [#144413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144413).
- Reduce repeated recipient expiry scans during live tool updates [#145581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145581).
- Reduce temporary allocations delivering agent events [#146077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146077).
- Reduce processing delays for large streamed code and tool-argument previews [#146236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146236).

<Accordion title="Ask a side question without losing the conversation">

Side chat puts the cursor in the right input when you open it and keeps the main conversation visible. Questions now wrap and grow as you type, with **Shift+Enter** for a new line and the existing 400-character limit.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose models while discovery continues">

Open the model picker from choices already loaded while discovery continues; newly found models appear in the open menu. Models settings also keep healthy choices usable when another provider fails, with a warning and Retry available. Account labels help distinguish subscription access, API keys, and an explicitly selected account. People with permission to edit a session can also change its thinking level and fast mode for subsequent turns.

<Accordion title="Review changes and repository state">

Activity now shows available Git change counts and pull-request previews, so you can check coding work without opening every conversation. Review also restores missing patches for filenames containing spaces or escaped characters. Activity counts describe the checkout or pull request, including applicable uncommitted work. Recent pull-request links from the same repository remain available after checkout changes, and the check-status control is easier to recognize and open.

- Make pull-request check details easier to open with mouse or keyboard [#147279](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147279).
- Skip redundant Git commit counts when validated remote branch tips match, while retaining visible uncommitted and unpushed work [#147508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147508).
- Reduce empty space between pull-request controls and the composer while retaining separation around intervening cards [#147474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147474).
- Combine remote-reference reads to avoid an extra Git process during session pull-request refreshes [#147443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147443).

- Retain recent same-repository pull-request links after checkout changes, with stale-status warnings during rate limits [#147166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147166).
- Avoid repeated transcript searches and saved-prompt decoding during unchanged session pull-request polling [#147373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147373).

<Accordion title="Use images and documents from chat">

Markdown attachments open as formatted documents beside the conversation, with headings, tables, and working code controls. Chat menus can copy available message Markdown, complete code, tables ready for a spreadsheet, or an attachment’s download link or name. New generated images stay with their completion reply without adding a duplicate image-only message. Uploaded image frames now align with their prompts, including in Safari, and user image galleries sit closer to the text that explains them.

You can also right-click chat images for the browser’s familiar copy and save actions. The browser may copy the displayed thumbnail. Use OpenClaw’s **Copy image** action when you want the full-resolution file.

<Accordion title="Keep dashboards and embedded views usable">

Ask your agent to add a hosted HTTPS website to a [session dashboard](/web/dashboards), expand it across the task area, and return through a named, pinned session. The browser also remembers layouts, panel choices, and dashboard tabs for up to 500 sessions.

Dashboard editors can use **Layout → Use current view as default** to save a shared fullscreen or split starting view, and agents can save it too. A viewer’s personal layout takes priority, and saving a default never rearranges active viewers. Fullscreen fills the task area, rather than the browser screen.

Some sites block embedding or need a separate sign-in; **Open website** opens them in another tab. Personal layout preferences, docking, and dimensions stay in this browser, and a live website frame is kept only while its task view remains cached.

<Accordion title="Inspect current usage details">

Enabled [usage footers](/concepts/usage-tracking) keep available totals and cache counts even when separate input and output counts are missing. When a session is recreated, Usage clears the old timeline selection so it no longer hides the new conversation’s messages. Hour filters also handle daylight-saving changes without freezing, and Models settings labels its usage-record count as messages. These display fixes leave billing totals unchanged.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Open and switch busy conversations">

Long conversations and chats with many child sessions do less repeated work when they open. The selected conversation now takes priority over automatic background session lists. Large session lists also give other requests a turn while preparing results, and chat startup avoids delays caused by WebSocket compression on busy Gateways.

Panes showing the same conversation share history recovery, and activity elsewhere avoids unnecessary redraws. Home and System busyness show compact, named loading dialogs that you can close and explicitly reopen once loading finishes.

The connection now sends uncompressed frames, so large histories and rosters use more bandwidth.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce unnecessary work when loading large tool-result histories [#137766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137766).
- Load Sessions query code only when needed [#145430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145430). Thanks @obviyus.
- Reduce repeated model selection in large session lists [#145580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145580).
- Reduce repeated history scans when matching final replies [#145618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145618).
- Reuse file checks across live history connections [#145696](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145696).
- Reduce work when reopening active chats near history limits [#145737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145737).
- Reduce repeated store checks when loading Sessions [#145748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145748).
- Avoid duplicate message preparation during history catch-up [#145885](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145885).

<Accordion title="Read current controls in your language">

<Accordion title="Browse chat files in tabs">

Keep several [chat files](/web/control-ui/chat) open in tabs and switch between them without losing your reading position. HTML files offer **Preview** and **Source**, and reopened editable drafts can save or resolve conflicts directly from Preview. Strict mode keeps document scripts disabled and applies the guarded preview’s resource restrictions.

Tabs clear when you reconnect. HTML previews retain the 256 KiB limit and do not load relative assets from the file’s directory; attachments from another origin remain download-only.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Keep multiple chat files open in tabs and switch HTML between Preview and Source [#146672](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146672).
- Reduce empty space above sidebar file previews [#147116](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147116).

**Bug fixes**

- Restore scrolling to the end of long file previews and retain position across tabs [#147115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147115).

**Security and trust**

- Apply guarded resource restrictions to strict HTML previews and restore saving and conflict recovery directly from Preview [#146873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146873).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect Gateway connection and resource graphs">

Settings → Gateway now shows connection ping statistics beside CPU, memory and activity graphs, helping you investigate a slow connection or a busy Gateway. Readings and uptime stay visible during refresh. Ping includes Gateway handling time and does not measure how long a model takes to reply; graph history resets when you leave or reconnect.

Full Debug status, health and model snapshots refresh when opened, when the connection or agent changes, or when you choose **Refresh**. Heartbeat and command activity stay live.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Hide conventional Linux EFI mounts from Gateway disk cards [#146739](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146739).
- Keep Gateway readings and uptime visible during refresh with inline first-load placeholders [#146740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146740).
- Show connection ping statistics alongside Gateway resource and activity graphs [#146755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146755).

**Bug fixes**

- Show when Gateway host statistics are loading [#145328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145328).
- Restore Gateway uptime in the Debug overlay [#146960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146960).
- Use lighter resource polling and refresh full Debug snapshots on entry, context changes or Refresh [#147110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147110).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Undo controls available">

Notifications pause their expiry timer while you hover over them or focus their controls with the keyboard, giving you time to use **Undo**. The remaining time resumes once both hover and focus leave, and you can still dismiss the notification immediately.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip unused browser measurements during immediate notification dismissal [#147196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147196).

**Bug fixes**

- Pause notification expiry while hovered or keyboard-focused so Undo remains available [#146940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146940). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Continue an offline device's conversation on the Gateway">

**Continue on Gateway** now recovers an offline device’s conversation even when pending workspace results previously blocked the action. After recovery, you can send a fresh message or delete the session with its transcript archived.

You must explicitly confirm the loss of unsynced device work. Ordinary moves continue to preserve pending results. Cleanup stays pending until the old device acknowledges its stop, so a recovered conversation does not prove that device has stopped.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore confirmed Continue on Gateway for offline-device sessions blocked by pending workspace results [#147390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147390). Thanks @obviyus, @masterswords1, @jalehman.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Catch up with Activity recaps">

[Activity recaps](/web/control-ui/settings#activity-tab) help you catch up on a conversation without reopening its full history. These rolling summaries survive page reloads, Gateway restarts, and archival. Previous text stays visible while a recap refreshes or fails, with freshness feedback and **Retry recap** when available. A recap is a convenience for catching up, not proof that a task is complete.

Generating recaps sends the previous recap and bounded transcript excerpts through the owning agent’s [utility model](/gateway/config-agents/models#agents-defaults-model). These separate model calls may incur usage charges. Disabling utility routing stops new recaps, and a failed call does not fall back to the primary model.

Read-only viewers can read cached recaps without requesting model work. Incognito and subagent sessions are excluded.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show persistent rolling Activity recaps with retained text during refresh or failure, permission-aware generation and utility-model routing [#147441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147441).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Updates and Maintenance

OpenClaw adds controls for older conversation storage and makes update, backup, and repair results easier to act on.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Archive older conversations and inspect storage">

Settings now shows where conversation storage is going, with per-agent details and a Run now control. [Cold storage](/gateway/config-agents/sessions#cold-storage) starts off; once enabled, it compresses eligible inactive history after 30 days by default, with policy changes applying without a restart. Open or resume an archived conversation to restore its history and make it searchable again, even if you have since turned archiving off.

[Make and verify a backup](/install/backups#cold-transcript-backups) before upgrading. This release changes the conversation database even with archiving off, so [going back requires the matching older build and backup](/reference/database-schemas/agent-schema-history#cold-transcript-storage). Keep archive files with the database; supported backups include and verify them.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Inspect storage and archive inactive conversations, preserve account-picker focus, avoid mutable startup during updater probes, and order Codex steering history after transcript projection [#145172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145172).

**Bug fixes**

- Recover artifact reads from concurrent transcript archival [#145625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145625).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose whether an update repair agent runs">

Owners can ask their agent to update OpenClaw using the default tool profiles, or use `/update` when model tools are unavailable. Optional owner setup remains an explicit choice. If an interactive update fails, its [AI repair prompt](/cli/triage) now requires Yes before using your account and tokens. Enter, No, cancellation, or a 30-second timeout skips repair and leaves diagnostics and [manual recovery instructions](/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery). An older updater already running may still use its old prompt.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add optional explicit first-owner enrollment during channel setup [#145527](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145527).

**Bug fixes**

- Let owners request updates through default tool profiles [#145573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145573).
- Explain owner permission and manual recovery when updates are refused [#146476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146476). Thanks @potterdigital.

**Security and trust**

- Require affirmative consent before interactive update repair starts [#146693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146693). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev, @as76.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve linked files in backups">

Backups preserve external, cyclic, and dangling symbolic links, and Mac metadata files no longer stop backup creation. Hardlinked names for managed SQLite databases receive the same committed contents, including changes still in their journal. All such hardlinks must be included in the managed inventory; incomplete or conflicting cases are refused.

Symbolic links retain their original targets without copying the files they point to. Review those targets before restoring elsewhere, and use an updated restore reader for these newly supported link archives. See the [backup guide](/cli/backup).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Mac metadata files from blocking backups [#140989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/140989). Thanks @ericcaiwx-star, @obviyus, @pollybot13.
- Preserve external symbolic links in backups with explicit restore warnings [#141925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141925). Thanks @adele-with-a-b, @obviyus, @vsumner, @clawputerlabs, @rayseling.
- Preserve committed data across SQLite backup hardlink names [#144624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144624). Thanks @obviyus, @aron-intframe.
- Preserve cyclic and dangling links in backups [#146161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146161). Thanks @obviyus, @adele-with-a-b.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep stale queued messages from replaying after upgrades">

Upgrades hold back abandoned messages from older file-based queues instead of sending them when a channel reconnects. This applies to pending messages at least 72 hours old and those with missing or invalid timestamps, including future dates. It does not change ordinary message expiry.

Review withheld messages in the private `.migrated` backups and verified queue-owned attachments in `.media.migrated`, then send a new message deliberately. Do not restore old pending entries to resend them. Finish any reported attachment preservation and cleanup before downgrading; `openclaw doctor --fix` on the same state directory retries incomplete cleanup.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Withhold stale legacy queued messages during upgrades and preserve them for review [#146583](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146583). Thanks @iwhatsskill, @scottrawson-sketch.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Finish core updates with clear plugin outcomes">

Core updates can keep a compatible installed plugin when its replacement is unavailable, with clear instructions for anything still needing repair or consent. Eligible old official-plugin release pins, including qualifying manual pins, can return to normal catalog tracking; an explicit version or tag in your current update command still takes precedence. Explicitly linked local plugins keep their selected source, even when a bundled plugin has the same name; follow the warning to update them at their source. A completed core update can therefore still need plugin follow-up. The [update guide](/cli/update/how-updates-run) explains these outcomes.

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Keep OpenClaw plugin repair commands copyable [#144808](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144808). Thanks @romneyda.
- Keep core updates moving when compatible plugin updates are unavailable [#145045](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145045). Thanks @blaekmajik, @noma-marketplacedb, @zhangmianzhong-glitch.
- Preserve matching source-built plugins through updates and Doctor [#145356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145356). Thanks @donniefi.
- Preserve explicitly linked local plugins during core updates and explain where to update them [#147009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147009). Thanks @obviyus.
- Complete bundled plugin files on the first Git update [#145664](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145664).
- Preserve custom-plugin sibling dependencies in private update rehearsals [#146616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146616). Thanks Jenna Brinning.

**Documentation**

- Explain updates with pending plugin consent [#145970](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145970).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Recover eligible official plugin release pins and report plugin repairs separately from core updates [b6073553b799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b6073553b799d81cb4805cc6111e73545d9b5d24). Thanks @finn763, @shakkernerd.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Update the installation you actually use">

Updates recognize custom npm installations, handle affected pnpm package-directory changes, and check build identity before skipping an explicitly selected same-version package. Git updates can prepare their required files before stopping service, and Mac package replacement preserves launcher permissions.

Your installed updater handles the first step. If an older version hits one of these blockers, follow the [manual update instructions for your installation](/install/updating/update-methods). If a baseline scan times out, the update retains a warning that rollback verification was reduced.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Unblock pnpm 12 global update staging [#144713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144713). Thanks @xilopaint.
- Continue npm updates after baseline scan timeouts with explicit rollback warnings [#144758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144758).
- Complete pnpm package-directory transitions and verify recovery before restart [#144778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144778). Thanks @obviyus, @xilopaint.
- Install explicit packages even when version numbers match [#144894](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144894). Thanks @romneyda.
- Explain skipped updates and the correct installation-specific next step [#145193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145193). Thanks @robotx5.
- Preserve macOS launcher permissions through updates and rollback [#145282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145282). Thanks @pollybot13, @yuxiao345, @alexph-dev, @oktrev, @rileyjjy.
- Compare build identity before skipping same-version package artifacts [#145349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145349). Thanks @nmotyl.
- Stage Git update objects before downtime and verify safe recovery after import failure [#145501](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145501). Thanks @romneyda, @fuller-stack-dev.
- Recognize custom npm installations for updates [#146091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146091). Thanks Rex Horizon.
- Avoid lint memory exhaustion during Git updates [#146186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146186).
- Restore Gateway after verified pnpm package-directory replacement [#146395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146395). Thanks @obviyus, @timetrvlr.
- Allow longer pre-update package scans on slow disks while retaining recovery limits [#144925](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144925). Thanks @njuboy11, @vincentkoc, @richard355168, @highsensor, @jaysonsolis, @baumus.
- Preserve the selected local file or URL during repeated managed updates [#146756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146756).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Retain local package changes through updates">

Package updates save supported local edits for recovery, and `--reapply-local-overrides` lets you explicitly replay trusted changes against matching original files. Fresh-profile updates now honor that choice too. Back up your edits and data before the first upgrade from an older updater. Recovery bundles are separate from data backups, remain on disk until you remove them, and keep conflicting changes available for manual recovery.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Save local package edits for recovery after updates [#89985](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/89985). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Honor local-override replay during fresh-profile updates [#145754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145754).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Check updates without disturbing live work">

Once the corrected updater is installed, update checks account for large databases and plugin folders when choosing temporary storage and allowing time to finish. They avoid copying entire conversation histories for simple version checks, leave copied background tasks idle, and preserve live Workshop files. Private checks also retain authentication policy and avoid the running MCP Apps sandbox port.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

<Accordion title="Understand update failures and recovery">

Failed updates preserve the original error alongside any later repair or cleanup failure, and give you a clearer next step. Supported recovery can continue under the updater that started it and restore the service that updater stopped. Keep preserved originals until you have checked the result. A recovery check is not a completed rollback, and a failed rollback may leave earlier saved changes in place.

Stalled activation now reports a timeout instead of waiting indefinitely. The Gateway can remain stopped while unfinished update work still owns it. Check `openclaw update status` and Doctor's guidance, wait for the updater and its child processes to stop, and follow the reported repair step.

Doctor refusals during affected older Git updates now provide manual recovery commands when the previous revision can be verified, or ask you to inspect Git history when it cannot. Wait for the updater to exit and use an independent terminal; restoring source alone is insufficient after data repairs. Update reports also distinguish an unavailable version reading from a real mismatch, and separate saved recovery advice from a Gateway responding now. That response does not establish that the update succeeded or make rollback safe.

<Accordion title="Restart services and recover with Doctor">

Doctor and service checks handle more Linux restart and inspection cases, including temporary session-bus loss after a manager has been identified. Repairs retain referenced environment credentials and can bring back a service that was running before maintenance, while leaving an already-stopped service off.

Windows stop and restart can continue after a broad process lookup fails when individual checks still identify the intended Gateway. Linux diagnostics explain when leftover child processes block maintenance; have their owning service or process administrator stop them. If Doctor warns that Windows state is in OneDrive, stop the Gateway, move the whole state directory to local storage, update its service environment, then restart and rerun Doctor.

Native shutdown can abandon unfinished calls when its deadline arrives, including after five seconds of draining on macOS launchd. An older running process still uses its old shutdown code. Where an external service manager owns OpenClaw, follow the [Doctor recovery instructions](/cli/doctor/recovery) to stop and restart it through that manager. Keep any preserved repair files until you have verified the result.

Restart preserves a recorded Gateway that is still starting and can target a verified healthy Gateway you launched manually, even when a Windows task is installed. If that process is unhealthy or cannot be identified, OpenClaw leaves it running with guidance. External supervisors and other tasks still need their own stop and restart controls.

<Accordion title="Recover and isolate agent database problems">

A database ownership conflict in a secondary agent can now leave healthy agents available. The affected agent stays offline until you repair it and restart OpenClaw; default-agent or shared-database failures can still block startup. Doctor can also preserve a verified misplaced duplicate before creating the correct empty database, and refresh registrations left incomplete by an interrupted upgrade.

Doctor also continues independent repairs when older agent folders contain conflicting files, preserving current files and existing SDK conversations. Databases awaiting relocation stay at their original location with their companion files. Inspect retained quarantines before removing them.

On the affected Bun SQLite worker path, four distinct databases can be open at once. Use the [supported Node path](/install/bun-compatibility) if you need more.

<Accordion title="Clean up large session stores">

Large conversation stores avoid repeated metadata reads during cleanup, and bulk cleanup no longer hits the reported stack-overflow crash. Cleanup can wait through brief database contention, while long transcript rewrites do less repeated search-index work. Reused cleanup workers and pauses between phases leave more room for other activity. Automatic retention also waits for ongoing session changes to finish, with existing retention rules and protections for active conversations kept in place.

<Accordion title="Verify large databases with less repeated work">

Large databases do less repeated checking during ordinary use. OpenClaw can reuse a successful check of the same file for the current Gateway lifetime, while fresh processes, Doctor, backups, and explicit maintenance retain full checks. Background inspections and temporary-file cleanup also leave more room for other work.

Damage introduced after a file was checked may be detected by normal database access, the daily verifier, or explicit maintenance instead of the next reopen. Full integrity checkers use a larger temporary cache, with about 64 MiB more peak memory per active checker in the recorded measurement.

If an inspection exhausts its ten attempts, the error now explains the limit and suggests waiting for database writes to settle before retrying. Ongoing writes are a possible cause, and the message does not guarantee the next attempt will succeed.

<Accordion title="Read and write session storage efficiently">

Session storage avoids repeated connection setup and extra row collection, with fewer unnecessary file-permission writes on macOS and Linux. SQLite workers can also accept larger supported writes through smaller transport chunks. For older installations, offline Doctor recovery can restore searchable conversations omitted from a legacy registry when their transcript files remain available, while respecting explicit deletions.

Conversation saves and model changes wait for database maintenance and cleanup shutdown before writing. Canceled conversation changes stay canceled.

<Accordion title="Save current settings and recover failed writes">

Settings saves now use the revision actually committed, including changes in included configuration files. In-flight form reverts are preserved, and stale included-file drafts receive a reload conflict. Save results distinguish settings written to disk from changes applied to the running app.

Failed staging preserves existing backup history, and failed saves can restore the original file when ownership checks allow it. If restoration is not confirmed, Settings keeps your draft, names the configuration and backup to inspect, and pauses coordinated UI saves. Discarding the draft requires a successful reload of an existing valid file. After a reconnect, successful Reload Config also resumes autosave; failed or offline reloads keep the draft.

[Doctor repairs](/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) now preserve supported nested model and agent settings without flattening included files. Removing an explicit included default model policy keeps an empty policy so later aliases do not unexpectedly restrict model access. A later update failure can leave the candidate package installed after those include changes, so retain the native backups and follow the repair guidance.

<Accordion title="Find the cause of slow or failed maintenance">

Status checks avoid unnecessary copies of large conversation databases and reuse session reads. Optional [diagnostic timelines](/cli/status) show where status collection spent time, while storage logs identify slow transcript reads and cleanup. Doctor also explains the fixed `USER.md` size limit and useful ways to shorten or move excess information.

Deep status and Doctor now warn when pending database writes cannot finish moving from the journal into the main database. Capture the status output and restart gracefully if directed. Never delete a WAL file to clear the warning; it can contain committed data. Status polling also reads task summaries with less interference in chat work.

- Reuse compiled plugin Doctor checks in built checkouts [#144676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144676).
- Break down delays in task cleanup and Git mutation logs [#144697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144697).
- Separate database integrity-check time from surrounding delays [#144746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144746).
- Avoid unused diagnostic logging work with Prometheus enabled [#145576](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145576).
- Reduce repeated diagnostic scans during plugin inspection [#145815](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145815).
- Batch session reads for health summaries [#146177](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146177).
- Reuse status reads and expose optional collection timings [#146228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146228).
- Reuse query preparation when collecting transcript usage statistics [#146281](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146281).
- Report external and ArrayBuffer memory in Gateway diagnostics without double-counting overlapping counters [#141902](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141902). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Avoid counting all historical events for recent proxy-capture session lists [#146752](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146752).
- Summarize task history for status without loading complete retained records into chat-serving work [#147141](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147141).
- Avoid full agent database copies for ordinary Doctor and restart schema checks [0f748036332e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0f748036332e3c9cac62c8e14fd73931cdfba4a4). Thanks @vacinc.

**Bug fixes**

- Explain USER.md's fixed size cap and useful remedies in Doctor [#136967](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/136967). Thanks @xialonglee, @patrick-erichsen, @majik360, @pawel-kowalczyk.
- Reject blank audit time filters and limits; omit unused flags instead [#137335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137335). Thanks @alix-007.
- Use the selected model's context size in Doctor tool checks [#144332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144332).
- Clear obsolete log records after an observed file disappearance [#144360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144360).
- Keep piped skill-verification reports complete within the 64 MiB response limit [#144559](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144559). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Include worker requests in Gateway latency diagnostics [#145602](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145602). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Speed up Doctor checks for large recovery inventories [#145711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145711).
- Count active sessions beyond the first history page and disclose the 100-ID display limit [#146207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146207).
- Avoid unnecessary database copies during status checks [#146213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146213).
- Identify slow transcript reads and storage maintenance in diagnostics [#146231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146231).
- Log failed temporary database snapshot cleanup while retaining normal read results [#144558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144558). Thanks @sunnyshu0925.
- Report blocked checkpoints and their progress in deep status and Doctor [#146775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146775). Thanks @thegizmopro.
- Label saved conversations as stored instead of active in status [#146979](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146979).
- Preserve newly appended log records after a file is truncated and regrows during a read [#147311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147311). Thanks @alix-007, @vincentkoc.
- Show redacted configuration inspection errors in support summaries instead of incorrectly reporting a missing file [#147414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147414).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="See which build is running">

Git installations report the built version and point out when it needs rebuilding to catch up with the checkout. If an agent fails because OpenClaw was replaced while still running, the error explains that a restart is needed. Update settings also display an inferred Extended stable channel correctly.

Doctor now warns when the recorded build and checkout commits differ, with advice to rebuild and restart. The warning is advisory and stays quiet during updates. Refreshing Git update status also clears stale reminders and behind counts when the checkout is current or ahead.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Show inferred Extended stable update settings correctly [#138952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/138952). Thanks @marmar9615-cloud, @obviyus.
- Report the built version and flag stale Git checkout builds [#143538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143538). Thanks @romneyda.
- Explain agent failures caused by an outdated running installation [#143906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143906). Thanks @romneyda.
- Clear obsolete Git update cards, Inbox reminders, and behind counts after a current or ahead comparison [#142959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142959). Thanks @ly85206559.

**Improvements**

- Warn about recorded build and checkout differences in Doctor, with manual rebuild and restart guidance [#144499](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144499). Thanks @romneyda.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read documentation and release history">

Documentation fixes make code examples and command labels readable in titles, preserve existing section links, and clarify setup and troubleshooting guidance. Per-version release notes are easier to browse, with labeled formatted and plain-Markdown reading options, including the earlier v2026.9.4 notes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore documentation syncing and preserve existing section links [#146415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146415). Thanks @hannesrudolph.

**Documentation**

- Correct documentation links and complete context-pruning guidance [#144133](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144133). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Simplify Why OpenClaw while keeping existing deep links [#144344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144344).
- Restore code and status text in documentation titles [#144389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144389). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Render complete documentation labels containing angle brackets [#144390](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144390). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Correct repeated wording about personal account labels [#144405](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144405). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Remove duplicate message-prefix migration guidance [#144409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144409). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Correct the Prometheus token-rate example to tokens per second [#144410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144410). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Browse per-version changelogs and complete Markdown mirrors, with links replacing oversized packaged copies [#145464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145464). Thanks @hannesrudolph.
- Mirror v2026.9.4 release notes in plain Markdown with reading-format links [#146386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146386). Thanks @hannesrudolph.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Back up application databases">

Backups can complete when unrelated application databases contain broken references between records, while OpenClaw's managed databases and declared plugin databases still receive checked snapshots. Agent and plugin discovery works during concurrent writes, including when workspace files are excluded. If discovery fails, resolve the error before creating a state backup. `--only-config` saves just the active JSON configuration file, excluding included files, state, and external credentials.

Unrelated databases and their companion files are copied as ordinary files with warnings. Use the owning application's backup procedure when you need a consistent copy of a running database or removal of deleted data. OpenClaw v2026.9.4 cannot restore archives containing these database companion files. Follow the [backup guide](/cli/backup), use `openclaw backup create --verify`, and keep a [verified pre-upgrade backup](/install/backups) if you need to downgrade.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep unrelated SQLite files from blocking backups, with warnings and changed restore-reader requirements [#146700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146700). Thanks @obviyus, @clawputerlabs.
- Preserve agent and declared-plugin discovery during concurrent writes and refuse incomplete state backups [#146912](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146912). Thanks @obviyus.
- Name the working backup creation command in recovery guidance [#147023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147023). Thanks @zzs12345-web.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Allow more time for slow updates">

Updates on slower machines and installations with large databases now allow time based on the work they need to complete. Ongoing copy progress extends the wait, and update-status network checks default to 300 seconds with explicit overrides still available. Larger allowances can mean longer waits during a stall, and an updater already running keeps the timers it loaded. A timeout does not make rollback or restart safe by itself; follow the reported recovery instructions.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Give slow updates work-based time allowances and preserve selected Chrome-tab navigation through stale loading and same-group events [290613f538dd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/290613f538dddfae9a90720c4ad559a2a5b330cf). Thanks @rlosito.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use configured deadlines for setup and repair">

Setup and repair conversations now use your configured agent timeout instead of stopping after two minutes. The embedded helper also skips skills it cannot use. Set an explicit shorter `agents.defaults.timeoutSeconds` value when you want to limit these runs; the documented default is 48 hours, and `0` disables the deadline. See the [setup and repair guide](/cli/openclaw).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Honor configured setup and repair deadlines and skip unusable embedded-helper skills [#147422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147422).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Messaging

Messaging updates make room conversations easier to follow, give you more ways to answer your agent, and improve how channels handle interrupted work.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Let specialists participate in a room">

Experimental room teams let you bring several configured agents into one conversation, address a particular specialist, and allow a limited follow-up discussion. Discord and Slack replies and attachments now stay with the participant who answered. Extra rounds use more agent runs, and the discussion's active budget does not survive a restart. See [broadcast groups](/channels/broadcast-groups) for setup and limits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Configure bounded multi-agent discussions in messaging rooms, with participant selection and shared WhatsApp roster handling [#141411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141411).

**Bug fixes**

- Deliver Discord and Slack group replies from the responding participant [#145735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145735).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use Discord voice and interactive replies">

Configured [GPT-Live conversations in Discord](/channels/discord/voice-channels#gpt-live-in-discord) can play short and subsequent replies, preserve pauses, and hear you interrupt while the agent speaks. Uneven audio arrives with fewer playback gaps, using up to 120 ms of initial buffering, and silence no longer interrupts the reply. Buttons, forms, model menus, and Activity launches also keep their actions tied to the intended choice.

For GPT-Live, remove `requireWakeName: true` and `consultPolicy: "always"`, or choose `gpt-realtime-2.1` for host-controlled turns. The separate opt-in [alternate Discord endpoint](/channels/discord) applies across accounts, blocks off-origin media, and does not support interactive voice.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Configure a confined alternate Discord API endpoint with no silent fallback to public Discord [#118067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/118067). Thanks @romneyda.
- Remove redundant input copies in Discord voice encoding [#145893](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145893).
- Reduce preparation time when splitting code-heavy Discord replies [#146074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146074).

**Bug fixes**

- Identify the correct permissions for Discord thread sends [#121144](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/121144). Thanks @alix-007.
- Honor Discord line limits for scheduled and other durable sends [#137969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137969). Thanks @anyech, @davidbennett1979, and @patrick-erichsen.
- Keep Discord model selections tied to an available runtime; reopen `/model` when an old menu expires [#143322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143322). Thanks @obviyus.
- Keep successive Discord widget clicks attached to the intended Activity [#145162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145162).
- Preserve resolved Discord account credentials and explicit plugin settings [#145424](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145424).
- Restore continuous GPT-Live replies, spoken interruption, and speaker-scoped delegated work in Discord voice [#146289](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146289).
- Wait for Discord button, select, and form registration and single-use cleanup [#146319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146319).
- Preserve short and uneven Discord voice playback, discard stale microphone audio after reconnect, and recheck permissions for delayed controls and delegated work; Copilot cannot steer an active run [#146798](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146798).
- Finish waveform processing before releasing temporary audio after a failed Discord voice send [#146885](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146885).
- Update Discord and OpenAI voice codecs to repair 40 ms and 60 ms stereo encoding while retaining the normal 20 ms production frames [#147093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147093).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Send richer LINE replies">

[LINE](/channels/line) can bring photos sent together into one turn so your agent can consider the set, and supported questions in direct chats offer answer buttons. You can also enable native quoted replies or receive completed portions of an answer as they finish.

Automatic quoting is off by default. Sending reply blocks uses more of LINE's message quota. For models without native vision, photo sets need `tools.media.image.attachments.mode` set to `all` and a sufficient `maxAttachments` limit.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Answer eligible agent questions with buttons in LINE direct chats [#133421](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133421). Thanks @edenfunf and @patrick-erichsen.
- Enable native quoted replies in LINE [#142092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142092). Thanks @edenfunf and @patrick-erichsen.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep invalid LINE carousel replies readable through text fallback [#113366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/113366). Thanks @edenfunf, @vincentkoc, and @patrick-erichsen.
- Handle LINE photo sets together and tell the agent when a known part is missing [#132136](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132136). Thanks @edenfunf and @patrick-erichsen.
- Explain missing or disabled LINE webhooks at startup and during probes [#133649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133649). Thanks @edenfunf and @patrick-erichsen.
- Make LINE block streaming settings work at channel and account scope [#142145](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142145). Thanks @edenfunf and @patrick-erichsen.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Slack threads responsive">

Slack keeps one progress card per command, shows interactive choices once, and preserves the complete written answer when controls cannot be used. A duplicate event waiting in one thread no longer holds up unrelated threads, and compatible corrections can reach a queued turn once it starts running.

Supported [access](/channels/slack/access-control) and [reply settings](/channels/slack/messaging) now apply to new work without reconnecting. Replies already underway keep their original settings; account, connection, and other restart-owned changes still reconnect the Slack monitor.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce formatting work for long Slack replies with links and annotations [#144633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144633).
- Change Slack access and replies without reconnecting, including access revocation during pending lookups [#146202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146202).
- Skip unnecessary entity searches when formatting Slack text [#146629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146629).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep one Slack progress card per command [#143879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143879). Thanks @n1hility and @sallyom.
- Keep Slack and Telegram command descriptions stable on completion [#143880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143880). Thanks @n1hility and @sallyom.
- Honor proxy settings for Slack relays and stop on proxy failure instead of bypassing the configured route [#143881](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143881). Thanks @n1hility and @obviyus.
- Preserve complete Slack fallback replies without repeating native choices [#144653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144653). Thanks @obviyus.
- Keep Slack threads responsive and accept compatible corrections to running queued turns [bffda1f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/bffda1f9452113c1db771f694598c8e4ede380a0). Thanks @pash-openai.
- Preserve useful Slack diagnostics when error causes loop or nest too deeply [#145721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145721). Thanks @alix-007 and @altaywtf.
- Restore setup with the affected published Slack plugin after updating OpenClaw core [#147109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147109). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Telegram conversations and input in order">

Telegram conversation titles include known forum topic names, and earlier text reaches the agent before later long-message fragments. An authorized `/stop` clears your pending input in that chat or topic. Agents can also discover supported persistent subagent conversations again, keeping follow-ups with the same helper when binding is enabled.

Supported [access rules](/channels/telegram/access-control) and [reply settings](/channels/telegram/messaging) apply to new work without reconnecting the bot. Active turns keep their captured settings; token, connection, account, and native-command changes still require their usual refresh.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Change supported Telegram access and reply settings without reconnecting [#146129](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146129).

**Bug fixes**

- Include known Telegram forum topic names in conversation titles [#140983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/140983). Thanks @obviyus.
- Treat intentionally skipped Telegram messages as suppression rather than failure [#143583](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143583). Thanks @sunlit-deng, @obviyus, and @jerabinovich.
- Preserve Telegram message order and stop pending input [#144839](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144839). Thanks @obviyus and @xilopaint.
- Restore discovery of persistent Telegram subagent conversations [#145543](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145543). Thanks @nimarosa, @obviyus, and @jackpothan.

**Documentation**

- Explain Telegram's persistent-storage requirements, bounded retention, possible repeated processing after a crash, and plugin hooks that run after message acceptance [#147222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147222). Thanks @navras98, @vincentkoc, and @mehmetulutug.
- Clarify the message tool for Telegram button sends, the combined send-message and poll permissions, and explicit sticker enablement [#147229](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147229). Thanks @navras98 and @vincentkoc.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve Teams mentions, tables, and polls">

Teams replies preserve mention names containing escaped brackets or backslashes and correctly separate raw tables from code blocks in lists when table conversion is off. Votes submitted while older poll data is being imported are also preserved.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Correct Teams raw-table boundaries around code blocks in lists [#146464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146464).
- Preserve escaped display names in Microsoft Teams mentions [#146532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146532).
- Reduce Teams poll cleanup reads and protect votes during legacy import [#146575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146575).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover Matrix replies and preserve local files">

Background-generated Matrix media can arrive after the incoming request has ended, and recovered deliveries retain accurate reply information. Long messages and spoilers also need less repeated formatting work.

Failed migrations now leave unrelated nearby files in place. If startup stops during a migration, retain the local database, source files, and `.migrated` archives while fixing the reported permissions or file conflict and retrying. The [Matrix migration guide](/channels/matrix-migration) explains recovery.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse Markdown analysis when formatting Matrix spoilers [#145261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145261).
- Reduce repeated scanning of long Matrix messages and spoilers [#146604](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146604).
- Skip unused style restoration when preparing plain Matrix messages [#146652](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146652).
- Avoid parsing the same Markdown twice when preparing long Matrix replies with tables and spoilers [#147418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147418).

**Bug fixes**

- Distinguish malformed Matrix directory replies from empty results [#131718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/131718). Thanks @xu-kai-quan, @obviyus, and @shojikumaru.
- Preserve unrelated files after Matrix migration failures [#144883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144883).
- Keep background media delivery working after Matrix requests end [#144907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144907). Thanks @romneyda.
- Report accurate reply metadata after Matrix media recovery [#145173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145173).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Restore iMessage feedback and remote attachments">

After the iMessage bridge recovers, supported typing indicators and read receipts can return on the next incoming message using your existing preferences. Official external iMessage installs can also copy permitted remote attachments into OpenClaw again.

When iMessage catchup is enabled, startup recovers the oldest missed messages first within the existing 500-message window per chat and your configured age limit. Each startup still has its replay cap. Catchup does not repeat automatically or recover messages that earlier runs already skipped.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

- Load fewer unrelated modules for iMessage delivery [#146294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146294).

- Restore iMessage typing and read feedback after bridge recovery [#142626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142626). Thanks @omarshahine.
- Restore remote attachment staging for official external iMessage installs [#144048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144048). Thanks @liuwqgit, @obviyus, and @mlapida.
- Recover older missed iMessage messages before newer ones when enabled catchup reaches its per-startup replay limit [#147395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147395). Thanks @obviyus, @zhangguiping-xydt, and @yetval.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use saved Signal accounts and long replies">

Signal accounts with names such as `Work Phone` can start without running Doctor when they have their own configured phone number. Their own endpoint and access settings now take effect, so review those settings when upgrading. If deleting an account would activate another saved identity, OpenClaw leaves it running and names the conflicting entries for you to resolve. See [Signal account guidance](/channels/signal).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Answer questions in Mattermost">

Mattermost offers buttons for supported simple questions, so you can choose an answer without retyping it. Typing still works, and questions needing private or more complex answers stay in text. Model menus also reflect the account selected for the conversation.

<Accordion title="Save and import Nostr profiles">

Authorized paired dashboard users can save and import Nostr profiles without supplying another credential. Profile actions can also use a valid saved credential when the browser credential is rejected, and rejected requests show readable errors.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep eligible Buzz rooms connected">

An unavailable Buzz room no longer keeps other eligible rooms offline. Granting a skipped room the Bot role can bring it back without reconnecting healthy rooms, while startup and reconnect reuse saved room information to prepare the appropriate history.
