</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Find the next step when a room conversation stops">

When a room conversation has exhausted restart recovery, a reminder can explain how to start again. Use an authorized `/reset` or `/new`, or choose **Resume in new session** in WebChat when the model is locked. Starting fresh keeps the old transcript.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Explain recovery steps when room sessions reject messages after restart failure [#146363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146363). Thanks @chachi-max.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Finish saving and clearing approval choices">

Authorized operators can use **Allow Once** or **Deny** on OpenClaw change-approval cards in Discord and Slack, and late button responses no longer overwrite the final result. Slack can also finish the original pending card after a restart.

iMessage, WhatsApp, Signal, and Matrix now wait for approval controls to finish registering or clearing before reporting that operation complete. Plugin authors must await the updated registration and deletion methods and use a supporting host. If optional storage fails, the fallback lasts only until the process stops; see the [approval SDK guidance](/plugins/sdk-channel-plugins/approvals).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Await approval binding registration and cleanup across four channels; external target stores must return `Promise<void>` from `register` and `delete` [#146325](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146325).
- Restore Discord and Slack change-approval buttons and preserve final outcomes, including Slack's original pending cards after restart [#147152](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147152). Thanks @obviyus, @liuwqgit, and @anyech.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect and recover channel connections">

Checking one channel's status no longer depends on unrelated account checks succeeding, and explicitly started accounts remain visible while discovery catches up. Recovery after a computer freeze also continues through diagnostic errors while keeping manually stopped accounts stopped.

Affected channel replies keep working after live settings changes without a restart. Queued replies and attachment cleanup stay with their original storage, while delayed replies and discovered conversations respect changes to the agent handling them.

Message scripts must omit `--channel` when they want automatic selection. An empty value now stops the command instead of silently choosing a channel; see [message commands](/cli/message).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Include channel names and verified help paths in JSON listings [#93265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/93265). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Reduce archive-notification discovery work while preserving conversations eligible for memory indexing [#146140](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146140).
- Count pairing-notification subscribers without loading unrelated records on supporting hosts [#146889](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146889).

**Bug fixes**

- Show explicitly started channel accounts when discovery lags [#144503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144503). Thanks @ericcaiwx-star and @obviyus.
- Suppress repeated Bonjour warnings after interface removal [#144870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144870). Thanks @tzy-17, @obviyus, and @tontoko.
- Prevent the shared-module loading race that could stop channel startup when plugins load together [#145026](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145026). Thanks @meajuda365.
- Finish pending delivery queue reads before shutting down so the replacement service can resume queued messages [#145168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145168).
- Restore channel model switching when browser or meeting-plugin enablement settings are left at their defaults [#145904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145904). Thanks @obviyus, @tsuel-dev, and @jiajia021.
- Keep filtered channel status working when unrelated account checks fail [#146271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146271).
- Recover channels after host thaw despite diagnostic inspection failures [#146720](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146720).
- Preserve the configured channel in plugin send results and restore source loading of declared `llm-core` exports [#146619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146619).
- Keep delayed replies out of canceled or replaced waiting conversations and return them to ordinary incoming-message handling [#146866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146866).
- Keep queued sends, completion records, and attachment cleanup in the state directory that admitted them [#146917](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146917).
- Recheck current agent routing before saving discovered conversation addresses [#146927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146927).
- Record queued conversation replies in their original storage and refuse sends or bindings whose route or session is no longer authorized [#146984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146984).
- Keep affected channel replies working after live settings changes when a running monitor still holds its startup configuration [#147001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147001). Thanks @sunhsiao, @xiankun-liang, @lidashi1025, @bronyazaychik0328, @bruce-yii, @obviyus, and @cqforest123-wq.
- Deliver delayed agent announcements to the selected target session's channel after the sender finishes, including across runtime reloads [#147150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147150). Thanks @vincentkoc, @mikywang, @liuwqgit, @sunlit-deng, and @bruce-yii.
- Avoid false readiness and timeout errors after Signal or iMessage startup is canceled and its pending check returns [#147510](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147510).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Reject blank message channel arguments before dispatch [#144802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144802). Thanks @ly85206559 and @obviyus.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Prepare long replies with less repeated work">

Long lists, code-heavy replies, and streamed progress need less repeated scanning as OpenClaw prepares them for chat. These changes preserve the text and message boundaries, while Discord persona replies also respect the requested size limit after mentions are expanded.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated code-block searches when splitting long replies [#145254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145254).
- Format long reports with many lists more efficiently [#145665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145665).
- Prepare long newline-chunked replies without rescanning preceding text [#146242](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146242).
- Reduce code-region parsing in streamed progress text [#146628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146628).
- Skip unnecessary text conversion while checking incomplete reasoning tags in progress updates [#146751](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146751).
- Reduce repeated processing in Matrix delivery queues, Feishu message batches, and single-page Beam transcript reads [#146772](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146772).
- Avoid replacing already-normalized spaces when preparing long reasoning progress updates [#146776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146776).
- Reuse Markdown preparation for Zalo Personal replies when syntax protection leaves the text unchanged [#147488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147488).

**Bug fixes**

- Respect persona reply text limits and retain each chunk's reply target in delivery tracking [#144629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144629).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Clean up failed Feishu attachment downloads">

When a Feishu attachment cannot be saved, OpenClaw now attempts to close its download stream even if the failure happened before reading began. The original storage or timeout error remains available for troubleshooting.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Close Feishu media streams after failed saves [#145891](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145891).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read accurate QQBot setup warnings">

Valid QQBot direct-message settings no longer trigger contradictory advice to add a wildcard that the configuration does not allow. This corrects the warning while preserving your access settings.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Remove contradictory QQBot direct-message wildcard warnings [#144187](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144187). Thanks @ericcaiwx-star, @obviyus, @lamkan0210, and @yubingjiaocn.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Understand WeChat pairing limits">

The [WeChat guide](/channels/wechat) now explains that plugin version 2.4.8 uses its own sender list and QR-login fallback, so normal OpenClaw pairing approvals and revocations do not reliably control its chat access. Disable that plugin if you require those controls. This is corrected guidance; the plugin's behavior has not changed.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Documentation**

- Explain WeChat 2.4.8 pairing, revocation, and host-version compatibility limits [#144262](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144262). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Follow progress in channel replies">

Supported channel previews now show readable progress notes, so you can see what your agent is doing while you wait. Slack and Telegram also remove abandoned previews after tool work while retaining the final answer or useful failure notice. Slack keeps a preview when someone has replied to it, preserving that part of the conversation. Your existing preview settings still control what appears.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Remove abandoned Slack and Telegram streaming previews while retaining accepted answers, failure notices, and Slack previews with human replies [#147106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147106). Thanks @vincentkoc, @doomclaw, @sweetsophia, and @100yenadmin.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Mattermost thread participation current">

Mattermost keeps a thread's original participation expiry after a restart, so an expired conversation requires a mention again.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep messages with the selected destination">

Messages now keep the room or conversation selected through the active channel plugin. That prevents a saved account default from adding a conflicting destination and blocking the send.

</details>
</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Memory

Memory maintenance keeps more completed work available after interruptions, while search spends less time processing text it will not use.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Rebuild and manage saved memories">

With embedding caching enabled, interrupted memory rebuilds can reuse completed work after a retry or restart, and the previous published index stays intact until its replacement succeeds. Partially missing keyword indexes recover during normal initialization, while [dreaming reports](/concepts/dreaming) now explain why candidates were not saved to long-term memory.

With search synchronization enabled, an older index can rebuild automatically when you search after an upgrade. That first repair can take longer and make paid embedding-provider requests. After a rollback, a newer-format index is preserved and search stays paused until you upgrade OpenClaw or explicitly reindex. Checking status alone does not rebuild it.

[Forget](/cli/memory) also removes a session's influence on memory ranking, but does not erase its source transcript. Forgetting a nonempty selection clears that agent's entire embedding cache, so later indexing may make new provider requests. Unrelated published memories remain searchable.

Scripts using memory commands with `--json` now receive one parseable error when validation, execution, or historical-workspace cleanup fails. Historical commands report success only after cleanup completes; a failure does not undo memory data already written.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce work when building overlapping memory chunks [#145733](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145733).
- Skip unnecessary setup when indexing empty files [#146150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146150).
- Count large memory-forget selections with fewer temporary allocations [#146215](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146215).
- Avoid loading every memory source record before cleanup [#146283](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146283).
- Reduce repeated origin-metadata checks when opening memory indexes [#146494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146494).
- Skip unused conversation text when previewing and forgetting memories [#147315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147315).
- Remove selected archived memory sources in batches when forgetting sessions [#147451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147451).
- Record bulk session-forgetting exclusions with fewer database writes while keeping those sessions excluded from future indexing [#147504](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147504).

**Bug fixes**

- Reuse completed embeddings after failed memory rebuilds and preserve structured provider errors [#144404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144404). Thanks @jalehman, @louisone1, @gaodaabao, @niriakot, @dbostik, and @loshay1.
- Avoid double-counting daily memories after capped dreaming sweeps [#144611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144611). Thanks @jinliyl and @obviyus.
- Recognize embedding quota exhaustion before shortening error displays [#144619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144619).
- Remove forgotten sessions' ranking signals and preserve retry after write failures [#144622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144622). Thanks @jinliyl and @obviyus.
- Rebuild partially missing memory keyword-search indexes [#144641](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144641). Thanks @jinliyl.
- Correct Bun memory writes through symlinks on non-Windows systems and stabilize indexing order [#144700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144700).
- Restore memory search fallback for index-name collisions [#144783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144783).
- Explain rejected memory promotions in dreaming reports [#145458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145458). Thanks @migelizaga-rgb, @vincentkoc, @ctbritt, and @nocode-ananas.
- Repair older memory indexes during eligible searches and retain useful failure guidance [#146713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146713). Thanks @obviyus, @githoubi, and @itanyplus.
- Let indexing wait for session cleanup without blocking the Gateway, and finish accepted synchronization work before shutdown [#146760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146760).
- Preserve newer memory indexes after rollback and retain automatic-rebuild cost notices [#146908](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146908). Thanks @obviyus.
- Split memory embedding requests when Zhipu embedding-3 reports a supported whole-number item limit [0afc023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0afc02335308892d7efa3ccf7d981e912c6dd238). Thanks @ylcn91 and @vincentkoc.
- Return one JSON error for memory command failures, including historical-workspace cleanup, with exit code 1 and readable error diagnostics [#147366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147366).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Memory search responsiveness">

Large memory searches and note preparation run in background workers, reducing pauses for other Gateway work. Keyword, filename, and project-scoped searches do less unnecessary reading and sorting while preserving their result rules. Opening several matches from one file also shares its preview instead of fetching it repeatedly.

Session indexing now prepares conversations in background workers and keeps search chunks from crossing conversation resets. Normal synchronization rebuilds existing indexes once for this fix and can reuse cached embeddings.

When optional conversation indexing is off, recall guidance now points the agent toward available exact-history tools before suggesting a settings change. Indexing stays opt-in, and existing tool permissions still apply.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Load less discarded text during fallback memory search [#145693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145693).
- Speed up keyword searches in large memory indexes [#145739](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145739).
- Reduce repeated work when optional memory diversity ranking is enabled [#146120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146120).
- Speed up memory path and filename searches [#146153](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146153).
- Reduce Gateway pauses during memory search and note preparation, with cancellation and concurrent-forget checks [#146168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146168). Thanks @jackten.
- Rank Chinese, Japanese, and Korean memory results with less tokenization work [#146256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146256).
- Skip unrelated project bodies in later memory-recall batches [#146301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146301).
- Fetch less text for vector memory search snippets [#146380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146380).
- Read bounded note excerpts during fallback memory search [#146507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146507).
- Reduce one-shot hashing overhead in memory and Slack processing [#146686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146686).
- Fetch only the note text needed for filename-search snippets [#146891](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146891).
- Avoid creating a second complete conversation export just to calculate its indexing fingerprint [#147481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147481).

**Bug fixes**

- Guide conversation recall to available history tools and clarify memory-read errors [#144669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144669). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Reuse file previews across matching Memory search results [#145238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145238).
- Prepare session indexes in background workers, preserve conversation reset boundaries, and keep published search data after worker failures [#147234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147234). Thanks @datrab.
- Suggest available exact-history access when optional session indexing is disabled [#147328](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147328). Thanks @liuwqgit, @vincentkoc, and @danhayman.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Memory Wiki search and compilation">

Memory Wiki reuses page content it has already parsed when searching, reading, and compiling your vault, reducing repeated work. Obsidian command detection also skips same-named folders so they no longer hide a valid executable farther along the search path.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse parsed pages for Memory Wiki search, reading, and compilation [#146364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146364).

**Bug fixes**

- Skip same-named directories when finding the Obsidian command [#144681](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144681). Thanks @alix-007 and @obviyus.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Public types for embedding plugins">

Embedding plugin authors can use the [public SDK](/plugins/sdk-subpaths) types for asynchronous text batches instead of importing internal memory types. Batches remain per-file by default; combining a whole source requires explicit opt-in through `sourceWideBatchEmbed`.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Expose supported SDK types for asynchronous embedding batches [#129625](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/129625). Thanks @ekinnee and @patrick-erichsen.

</details>
</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Skills

The skill library and Workshop make it easier to see what you’re choosing and what happened when you changed it.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Find and load selected skills">

Available ClawHub skill artwork now appears in search results and detail views, making skills easier to recognize. Sessions with several pinned skills load their revision details together while keeping the exact versions you selected.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Load pinned skill metadata in one query while preserving selected revisions [#146235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146235).

**Bug fixes**

- Display ClawHub skill artwork in search and details [#123051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/123051). Thanks @romneyda.

**Documentation**

- Restore readable skill-creation tutorial steps [#144388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144388). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Apply Workshop suggestions with clear results">

[Skill Workshop](/tools/skill-workshop) shows confirmed Apply and Reject results promptly, keeps suggestion rows consistent through refreshes and agent switches, and dismisses temporary confirmations on time. If a result is uncertain, refresh and check before trying again. A changed suggestion still needs your review and another explicit action.

Doctor also points out automation paths left behind when Workshop moves a skill, so you can update the affected jobs yourself. When sandbox restrictions block Workshop, troubleshooting guidance explains that restriction before suggesting changes to the tool allowlist.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Warn about automation paths left behind by Skill Workshop relocation [#144680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144680). Thanks @obviyus and @rayseling.
- Dismiss expired Skill Workshop confirmations [#145015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145015).
- Avoid false Workshop relocation warnings in full Doctor lint [#145396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145396). Thanks @raydestar, @obviyus, and @githoubi.
- Explain sandbox restrictions before suggesting Workshop allowlist changes [#145546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145546). Thanks @deltaecho3.
- Keep Workshop confirmations and suggestion rows consistent through refreshes and agent switches, preserve reviewed content, and wrap mobile notices within the screen [56fcd66](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/56fcd66e484876f1eba1055fe6eb45f8b673006a). Thanks @obviyus and @yangxiansheng.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Prepare skills without blocking unrelated work">

Other Gateway requests can continue while OpenClaw checks skill requirements, prepares skill commands or reads sandbox files. This helps keep OpenClaw responsive when several agents are starting work.

Security scans use fresh findings after a skill file is replaced. If you edit a file during a scan and the scan fails, finish editing and retry. Oversized source files are skipped. If a newly loaded pinned skill is rejected because its instructions exceed 1 MiB or use a disallowed link, restore the skill artifact or explicitly detach the pin.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid unused work during single-workspace skill loading [#145151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145151).
- Reduce missing-command lookup work for Unix skills and hooks [#145175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145175).
- Check shared missing skill dependencies once per status report [#146274](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146274).
- Skip unrelated file activity earlier in the skills watcher [#146309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146309).
- Keep Gateway requests responsive while agents prepare skill requirements [#146373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146373).
- Skip unnecessary skill scans for ordinary URLs when model aliases are configured [#146467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146467).
- Skip executable searches for skills and hooks with missing prerequisites [#146470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146470).
- Skip plugin discovery during skill preparation when plugins are disabled [#146615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146615).
- Let other Gateway work continue during skill-command checks, with higher preparation time and CPU use in measured workloads [#146812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146812).
- Skip executable searches for excluded or always-included skills while rechecking changes to their requirements [#147359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147359).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep sandbox file reads responsive, use bounded buffers for package and skill-support verification, enforce existing skill-card and literal-tilde script-preflight read limits, and keep skill scan findings and displayed metadata tied to current content [#146654](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146654).
- Enforce existing 16 KiB source-metadata and 1 MiB pinned-instruction limits during skill reads, preserve metadata fallback, and stage large worker bundles without loading whole artifacts into memory [#147336](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147336).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Connect Google Workspace from a remote host">

The Google Workspace skill now tells agents to try your existing sign-in before asking you to set it up again. Updated guidance explains how to complete sign-in from a trusted shell when OpenClaw runs on a remote computer. Keep callback URLs and secrets out of chat, and enter callback URLs yourself in that shell rather than passing them through an agent tool.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Documentation**

- Reuse working Google Workspace authorization and clarify operator-controlled sign-in on remote hosts [#145945](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145945). Thanks @donniefi, @vincentkoc, and @abhishek17n.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Create interactive visualizations">

The bundled `visualize` skill helps agents turn explanations into interactive charts, choose between a one-off visual and a saved dashboard, and update existing widgets. Widgets can now load chart libraries and fonts from [approved public hosts](/tools/show-widget#libraries-and-fonts), so agents can use tools such as D3 without packing the whole library into each visual.

Downloaded code can read the widget’s content and use permissions you have granted it. Loading an asset also shares your device’s IP address and the requested asset with its host. Access to dashboard data and actions on the OpenClaw host still require separate permissions.

Recreate older saved inline widgets to use these assets, and prefer URLs pinned to a specific library version. PNG exports may omit external styles and fonts, and strict embed mode still disables scripts.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add the visualize skill and allow widget chart libraries and fonts from approved HTTPS hosts [#147265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147265).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Native Apps

The native apps bring more of your work into the device you're using, from a local Gateway on your Mac to code review on Android and quick prompts from the Linux desktop.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Run and maintain a local Mac Gateway">

You can [run a Gateway on your Mac alongside a remote primary](/platforms/mac/remote#run-a-local-gateway-alongside-a-remote-primary), with separate chat and dashboard windows. Enable “Also run a Gateway on this Mac” in Connection settings. It is off by default, and your remote primary keeps its existing Talk, menu-bar and node role. Connection settings also offers install, update and repair actions for app-managed local Gateways, even after you dismiss setup.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Run a local Mac Gateway alongside a remote primary [#145038](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145038).
- Translate Mac Gateway setup and recovery messages [#144613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144613).
- Translate macOS local Gateway controls and port notices [#145311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145311).

**Bug fixes**

- Install, update, or repair the local Gateway from Mac Connection settings [#144554](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144554). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Avoid forced Mac Gateway reinstalls after inconclusive service checks and retain startup diagnostics [#146880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146880).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover Mac connections and switch Gateways">

The bottom-left account menu now shows Gateway health and switching controls, with hints for existing keyboard shortcuts. Use Command-click or Control-click to open another window. Expired Cloudflare sign-ins have a recovery page inside the affected window; windows restored at launch wait for you to click “Sign in again.”

If the approval page does not appear, choose “Open browser” and check the browser account before approving. A timed-out attempt needs a fresh sign-in. Closing a sign-in window opened by a command cancels that command.

[Nearby setup](/platforms/mac/remote#configure-in-the-app) keeps your saved connection until you enter a trusted address, setup code or SSH destination and choose Save. Cancel keeps the current connection, and Direct node pairing now requires explicit approval.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move Mac Gateway switching and health into the sidebar account menu [#145341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145341).
- Show Mac Gateway shortcut hints in the sidebar [#145416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145416).
- Translate Mac browser sign-in recovery messages [#145110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145110).
- Refresh Mac connection-diagnostic translations [#145856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145856).
- Keep the selected Mac Gateway's checkmark without a redundant shortcut hint [#146753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146753).
- Translate Mac Open browser and inactive sign-in recovery guidance [#147025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147025).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve Mac app identifiers despite unrelated plist metadata [#144381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144381).
- Recover expired Mac Gateway sign-ins inside the window [#145040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145040).
- Reopen a missing browser approval page for the current Mac Gateway sign-in [#146787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146787).
- Stop Mac dashboard reload loops after a profile port conflict [#145333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145333).
- Cancel stale Mac connection resets and diagnose the active tunnel port [#145427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145427).
- Ignore stale Apple Gateway discovery results after stop or refresh [#145435](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145435).
- Drag macOS windows from empty side-panel tab header space [#146404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146404).

**Security and trust**

- Keep Nearby discovery separate from saved Mac connections [#121622](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/121622). Thanks @jacobtomlinson, @eleqtrizit, @freeqaz-openai, @joshavant, @pgondhi987, and @vincentkoc.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Mac browser tabs with their chat">

Switching conversations now shows the Mac browser tabs belonging to that chat, and returning brings them back while the window remains open. Tabs can also show the page's own icon. This needs an updated Mac app and Gateway-served UI; older tabs stay shared until closed. Login cookies remain shared across chats in the same window.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show page-supplied icons on native Mac browser tabs [#145714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145714).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Mac browser tabs with the chat that opened them [#144508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144508). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Discard outdated Mac browser-import banners after mode changes [#145449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145449).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep an authorized Mac desktop available">

Authorized Computer work can keep an unlocked Mac awake for up to an hour from its first action. To keep it awake between jobs while connected and hosting, enable [Unattended desktop hosting](/platforms/mac/permissions) under Settings → This Mac and accept the native confirmation. That option is off by default. It does not bypass login or lock; after locking or losing access, sign in normally and start fresh Computer work.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Keep authorized Mac desktops awake, open session desktops directly, launch discovered Mac apps, restore node hosting after reapproval, and complete prepared-worker refill and cleanup [#144226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144226).
- Translate the Mac unattended-desktop consent dialog [#145055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145055).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose models and sign in on Apple devices">

iOS and Mac chat show the model, thinking and Fast controls supported by your current session, with provider sign-in available from chat. Mac Quick Chat refreshes choices without losing your draft and offers supported speed settings for the next message. If an older Gateway cannot supply native model choices, update it or use `/models` and `/login`.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Match Apple model controls to supported choices and add provider sign-in [#144762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144762). Thanks @obviyus.
- Refresh Quick Chat model choices and add speed controls [#144804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144804). Thanks @obviyus.
- Refresh Apple model-settings and sign-in translations [#144856](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144856).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Stop Mac audio and capture cleanly">

Cancelled local MLX speech no longer passes late audio to playback or interferes with a newer Talk request. Reopening the microphone test waits for the previous test to finish cleaning up, and camera capture ends before the recording is exported.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Discard late local speech after Mac Talk cancellation [#144586](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144586).
- Finish microphone-test cleanup and release camera capture before export [#145450](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145450).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Mac windows, commands and launch preferences">

Dashboard controls stay visible and clickable in full screen, including after reconnecting. Commands that finish quickly return their actual result instead of a false timeout, and rapid changes to launch-at-login preserve your latest choice.

Automation can also close an OpenClaw window without crashing the app or its other windows. Update or rebuild OpenClaw.app to receive this fix; updating the Peekaboo CLI alone is insufficient.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Mac Dashboard controls accessible in full screen [#144571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144571). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev, @shojikumaru, and @arong-me.
- Return actual results for fast Mac commands instead of false timeouts [#145437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145437).
- Preserve the latest Mac launch-at-login setting [#145451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145451).
- Stop obsolete Mac file notifications and release Canvas watchers [#145453](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145453).
- Close OpenClaw windows through native automation without crashing the app [#147114](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147114).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Review work and choose models on Android">

Open Chat actions → Review changes to inspect uncommitted code and add a selection to your existing draft without sending it. The viewer is read-only and appears when your Gateway supports change review. The Chat actions menu also stops closing unexpectedly on affected phones.

Model controls follow the selected model's supported Fast and thinking choices, and refresh failures show a warning with a way to retry. Provider sign-in and API-key setup are available from the picker when supported by your Gateway and administrative access; older Gateways show update guidance and retain slash commands.

- Review uncommitted changes and add selected code to Android drafts [#144101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144101). Thanks @ansxor and @iwhatsskill.
- Match Android model controls and sign-in choices to Gateway capabilities [#144711](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144711). Thanks @obviyus.
- Translate Android change-review controls and selection labels [#144501](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144501).
- Translate Android model-refresh retry guidance [#144698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144698).
- Refresh Android and Wear OS sign-in and model guidance translations [#144759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144759).
- Refresh Android sign-in and Mac Gateway connection translations [#145410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145410).

- Show Android model refresh warnings and reload choices on agent switches [#142355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142355). Thanks @obviyus.
- Keep Android Chat actions menus open on affected phones [#145505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145505). Thanks @donniefi and @iwhatsskill.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="End watch calls and choose models for the current chat">

Ending Talk on Wear OS starts microphone and speaker shutdown before waiting for the phone, while “Ending call…” keeps the conversation visible. Model choices now follow the chat selected on your watch, with available choices retained during a partial refresh. Update the phone app and Gateway if the picker reports missing support.

Replies stop waiting once their outcome is confirmed, including a completed answer or a successful Stop. The watch also replaces its displayed reply when the phone shortens or clears the text, so an earlier version does not linger.

Microphone permission controls offer Retry or Open Settings. After granting access, return to the watch app and tap again to start recording. If the microphone cannot start, Voice now shows an audio error.

- Recover Wear OS microphone permissions without starting recording automatically and clean up failed Talk audio [#144414](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144414). Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Show an audio error when Wear OS Talk cannot start recording [#147073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147073). Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Finish waiting after confirmed Wear OS reply outcomes and replace shortened or cleared streamed text [#144387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144387). Thanks @iwhatsskill.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Browse inside the Tauri companion">

The Tauri companion gains browser tabs beside your conversations, with navigation, screenshots, element inspection and native Save dialogs. Conversations keep separate tabs, but all reading tabs share cookies in one private browsing session. Closing every tab, switching Gateways or quitting ends that session; sign-in links still open in your system browser.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Browse, inspect and save pages inside the Tauri companion [#145572](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145572).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use the Linux tray and Omarchy bar">

The optional [Omarchy 4 bar panel](/platforms/omarchy) puts agents, sessions and quick prompts on your desktop, using the Linux app's selected Gateway when available. Install it separately with a compatible app or CLI. Drafts stay in memory and are lost when the shell restarts, and uncertain sends are not retried automatically.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Access agents, sessions and quick prompts from an Omarchy 4 bar panel [#145593](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145593).

**Bug fixes**

- Show OpenClaw in Linux tray listings [#146174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146174).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover Linux Gateway connections">

The Linux companion can retry your saved remote Gateway and return from Connection Settings without losing the active chat or its sign-in. It keeps reconnecting when service status is unknown and offers Start Gateway only after a confirmed stop. A failed sleep listener no longer leaves the app incorrectly stuck asleep with local recovery blocked.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Respect saved Linux connections and accept healthy Gateways with unknown service status [#146045](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146045). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Clear stale Linux sleep state when the system sleep listener stops [#146046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146046). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Retry saved Linux connections and a failed SSH tunnel once, preserve chats when leaving Settings, reuse credentials for the same address, and restore native access after local Start or Restart [#146047](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146047). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep Linux reconnecting during unknown service status and reserve Start for a confirmed stop [#146857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146857).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Retry Linux Quick Chat">

If Linux Quick Chat loses confirmation of a finished request, retrying the unchanged draft can retrieve its completed answer without asking the model again. The recovered reply clears the busy state so you can continue typing. If recent history is unavailable or contains no matching completed answer, the error remains.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recover completed Linux Quick Chat replies on unchanged retries and reject stale sends after switching Gateways [#146048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146048). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose agents and keep Mac chat drafts">

The native Mac chat sidebar now lists named agents above their threads, so you can switch conversations and return to each one's text draft. Activity and attention indicators help you find work that needs you, while timestamps and cached message previews make threads easier to recognize. These indicators cover loaded sessions and local history. Pending attachments still need to be handled before switching conversations.

New Thread keeps your selected agent and offers a separate Git working copy when needed. A quieter reading layout and a composer that grows with your draft leave more room for the conversation, with model sign-in close by when credentials need attention.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Navigate named Mac agents, retain conversation drafts, choose New Thread working copies, and use the revised transcript and model menu [#146531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146531).
- Translate native conversation navigation and separate-working-copy guidance [#146924](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146924).
- Translate iOS and Mac chat activity, unread counts and personal message-preview labels in 21 existing non-English locales [#147384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147384).
- Show Mac agent activity and cached thread previews, place sign-in by the composer, and distinguish finished, failed, empty and missing tool results [#147197](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147197).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use the companion's window controls">

The Tauri companion puts window controls in the dashboard header, with empty space for dragging and a double-click to maximize or restore. You can still resize from the window edges. Closing the main window leaves the app in the tray, while extra Gateway windows close normally. Older dashboards keep their native titlebars until updated.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Combine Tauri window controls with the dashboard header while preserving drag, resize, modal and older-dashboard behavior [#147097](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147097).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Follow Linux desktop updates">

Regular stable releases now start Linux desktop publication automatically. While a new bundle finishes, the updater can keep offering the previous signed AppImage. Linux downloads finish separately from the core release, so a new core version does not mean its desktop download is ready at the same time.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Start Linux AppImage and Debian publication with regular stable releases and preserve an available previous signed AppImage update [#147113](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147113).

</details>
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep answers visible in Mac chats">

In the full Mac chat window, completed work folds into an expandable row above the answer, keeping the result and its attachments in view. Active work, work without an answer and unresolved work after an answer stay expanded. You can open the row to inspect earlier steps, including failures followed by a valid answer. Find in Conversation reveals folded content, and transcript exports keep it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Fold completed work above full-window Mac chat answers and preserve separate progress messages through history refreshes [#147540](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147540). Thanks @cbruney.

</details>
</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Models and Providers

GPT Live reaches more voice conversations, while model settings give you clearer control over accounts, usage and the model doing the work.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Use GPT Live in Talk, meetings and calls">

You can speak while GPT Live replies in supported [Meet, Teams and Zoom meetings](/plugins/meeting-plugins), and let it consult your OpenClaw agent during meetings or [phone calls](/plugins/voice-call/realtime-and-streaming). Select Live explicitly for these surfaces. In [Talk](/providers/openai/voice-and-speech), unpinned sessions now choose Live using your configured OpenAI access. Existing model pins stay in place.

Live is audio-only, so choose `gpt-realtime-2.1` when you need a camera. Configured Platform credentials take precedence over ChatGPT subscription access and use the Platform billing route. For meetings, update both Gateway and paired node and use supported isolated audio capture; remove a custom `audioInputCommand` before selecting Live. Live does not support Voice Call custom functions or host-controlled wake-name and forced-consult modes. On Apple clients, Stop ends the Live session. Headphones help prevent speaker feedback.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Add public GPT-Live and choose unpinned Talk defaults by account [#145382](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145382).
- Reduce CPU work in shared voice and telephony audio conversion [#146469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146469).
- Extend GPT Live continuous voice and agent delegation to meetings and calls, with isolated capture and without native Voice Call custom functions or host-controlled wake-name and forced-consult modes [#146546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146546).

**Bug fixes**

- Correct spoken-update guidance for public GPT-Live [#145807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145807).
- Prevent microphone drift in GPT-Live relay calls [#146078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146078).
- Discourage repeated work in GPT-Live calls [#146182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146182).
- Fix repeated GPT Live captions and match spoken confirmations to the current action [#146370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146370).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Connect accounts and see their remaining usage">

Connecting a provider keeps your chosen model. Replacement sign-ins saved through Model Setup remain inactive until tested and explicitly activated, so a failed replacement leaves your working connection available. Administrators can also compare remaining Codex usage beside each saved subscription login on Models and refresh accounts individually. API-key and other-provider rows are outside that quota display. Stored accounts return to Ready after verified quota recovery without a Gateway restart.

Eligible automatic background OpenAI work preserves subscription billing when an API key is also present, while explicit metered account choices still win. Before downgrading OpenClaw, remove inactive replacement sign-ins or restore the state from before setup, because older versions may use them.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose models from current account lists">

Choosing Show all after sign-in reveals models already fetched from your account. Model lists can refresh expired provider catalogs in the background while keeping saved choices available, and a failed refresh leaves a warning you can retry. [Model permissions](/concepts/models) now match the exact provider and model, so explicitly allow the intended pair if an older accidental match stops working. Showing a model never grants account access to it.

If you declare a provider in current settings, supply its credentials and headers through the supported account or configuration settings. Keys and headers stored only in an older `models.json` no longer fill gaps in that declaration.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Show the answering fallback model and its actual context limit [#134496](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134496).
- Keep configured model catalogs and request settings consistent [#143837](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143837). Thanks @obviyus.
- Preserve selected models through fallback and credential refresh [#143961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143961).
- Keep model capabilities aligned with the configured connection [#144180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144180).
- Keep configured prices tied to the exact model entry [#144182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144182).
- Keep lightweight and worker requests on the selected model [#144238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144238).
- Preserve provider-prefixed model IDs in settings [#144275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144275).
- Stop unlisted models inheriting another model's output limit [#144292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144292). Thanks @ylcn91, @obviyus, @yetval.
- Normalize captured bare model defaults once [#144429](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144429).

- Validate the selected model without resolving its alias twice [#144474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144474).

- Avoid false model-change reports during settings saves [#144807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144807). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep discovered models available through same-account token renewal [#144861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144861). Thanks @obviyus.
- Discover account models after sign-in without blocking starter choices [#145190](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145190). Thanks @obviyus.
- Make newly discovered models usable on their first turn [#145726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145726). Thanks @obviyus.
- Handle unavailable startup accounts during model-list refresh [#145859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145859). Thanks @obviyus.

- Keep model discovery focused on relevant plugins and apply provider request wrappers, including custom headers, to direct prepared requests [#147159](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147159).
- Keep provider-specific model refreshes on the requested providers and their prepared plugin files [#147323](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147323).

- Avoid false Doctor missing-model warnings when a known provider has no offline catalog, and explain that offline validation is unavailable rather than confirming the model is valid [#145076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145076). Thanks @emes, @mnowrot.

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated model lookups while listing sessions [#137901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137901).
- Reduce processing during large model-catalog searches [#145139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145139).
- Skip unused provider models while reading configuration [#145204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145204).
- Reduce unnecessary loading during initial provider discovery [#145212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145212).
- Prepare large configured model lists with less repeated work [#145433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145433).
- Reduce repeated thinking-option lookups in large model lists [#145439](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145439).

- Reduce copying in CLI model lookups [#146180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146180).
- Reduce unnecessary runtime-policy lookup work [#146199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146199).
- Reuse provider-policy resolution when preparing model lists [#146657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146657).
- Reuse authentication directory resolution in automatic-runtime model catalogs [#146728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146728).
- Reduce repeated setup work when refreshing models for many agents [#147081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147081).

**Security and trust**

- Preserve selected models and enforce exact model permissions [#144041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144041).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Guide Codex while it works">

With a supporting Codex model, you can answer a question in the web UI while work continues, or return to it after the turn finishes. Suggested answers require your submission. When policy requires permission for a requester's connected MCP tool, Codex now asks you to allow that call once or deny it.

Local native Codex uses your existing Codex home unless isolation is requested, and older runtime pins may need explicit account import or reselection.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

- Explain why Codex receiver startup was cancelled [#144677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144677). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Explain Codex plugin health failures and recovery options [#144813](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144813). Thanks @romneyda.
- Preserve Codex subagent results through temporary storage failures [#145520](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145520).

**Improvements**

- Align native Codex sign-in with model choices and authentication status [#143588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143588). Thanks @obviyus.
- Answer Codex questions without stopping ongoing work [#144664](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144664).
- Identify OpenClaw tasks and triggers in Codex usage metadata [#145455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145455). Thanks @pash-openai.

Thanks @vincentkoc, @nvtoroy, @aspalagin.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use supported reasoning levels">

Fable 5 and 5.1 now inherit Medium reasoning effort, as does GPT-6 Astra when the account supports it. Your explicit effort settings still win. Fable and Mythos offer their supported Low through Max choices; update scripts that send `/think minimal` or `/think adaptive` to use `/think low` or `/think high` instead. The [thinking guide](/tools/thinking) explains model-specific defaults and how `/think default` clears a saved session override.

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Correct Fable and Mythos thinking choices and document directive changes [#145395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145395).

- Use Medium reasoning by default for Fable and supported Astra accounts [#145554](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145554).

- Explain existing per-model thinking defaults and precedence over the global default [#146040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146040). Thanks @smartnewb, @altaywtf.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep custom model settings in effect">

Custom OpenAI-compatible models retain the advanced reasoning levels they declare through session creation and request preparation, within the API's supported limits. Custom Anthropic and Mistral requests also preserve your requested response budget when model metadata omits an output ceiling. Provider limits still apply, and Anthropic manual thinking is disabled if it cannot fit that budget.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Understand model context limits">

Context checks now count tool definitions, making very short replies caused by a crowded tool catalog easier to diagnose. Affected OpenAI-compatible requests log a warning when almost no reply space remains. Custom model configurations below the existing 4,000-token minimum now stop before a request is sent, even if a larger input cap was configured. Correct the model's declared window or choose a model with enough room.

If you downgrade, older OpenClaw versions can discard saved model-budget information during recovery, leaving context engines to use their fallback limits.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Continue after a provider's progress message">

An agent can continue from a progress message to tools and a final answer when a Responses provider explicitly says the turn is unfinished. You no longer need to send another prompt in that case, and cancellation and stop controls still apply.

- Propagate cancellation through affected Bun HTTP response streams [#145598](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145598).

- Continue agent work after Responses progress messages that explicitly request another turn [#146393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146393). Thanks @vacinc.

**Improvements**

- Reduce copying when preparing Anthropic tool definitions [#145287](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145287).
- Reduce repeated processing of streamed HTTP replies [#145683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145683).
- Skip unnecessary large-number scanning for ordinary text tool arguments [#146241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146241).

- Skip unnecessary JSON reconstruction for streamed ordinary text and Unicode while preserving escape handling [#147101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147101).

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep image and PDF requests on the intended model">

Image descriptions and PDF analysis keep the model selected for that request, and PDF results name the selected model consistently. Heavy image and [PDF processing](/tools/pdf) can run separately so other OpenClaw requests continue and cancellation can take effect. Busy workers can reject new work, and their three-minute deadline includes time spent waiting.

- Honor `--agent` in CLI image describe and describe-many, using the selected agent's model and workspace context in multi-agent setups [#147456](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147456). Thanks @obviyus, @mertbasar0.

**Improvements**

- Decode fewer bytes when identifying supported Anthropic images [#145277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145277).

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve long voice notes and avoid wasted synthesis">

Deepgram Flux preserves voice-note audio beyond 20 minutes instead of silently dropping the end, within your configured size and timeout limits. The speech CLI also rejects an unsupported remote Gateway `--output` request before synthesis starts, avoiding provider usage for a file it cannot save locally. Audio transcription commands now identify the provider and model used when that information is available.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Connect organization models through Radius">

The new [Radius provider](/providers/radius) connects your organization's model access through browser sign-in or an API key, with streamed answers and supported image and tool use. Requests use the selected organization's credits and follow its budgets and policies. Available models depend on that access, and some advertised models can still be unavailable upstream.

**Bug fixes**

- Send valid Radius Claude requests with thinking disabled [#145565](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145565).

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="See why a search failed">

When every automatic web-search provider fails, operator logs retain the first failure to help you trace the problem. The docs CLI also reports malformed search responses as errors, so a broken response no longer looks like a successful search with no matches.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Retain the first error when all automatic search providers fail [#132755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132755). Thanks @tommyjoseph, @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Automatic Daybreak retries">

Eligible defensive-security requests can receive one automatic Daybreak retry after a structured OpenAI policy refusal, in both native Codex and embedded OpenAI chats. The retry keeps your selected session model in place. Successful interactive embedded retries name Daybreak or your configured retry model before the answer, including in the originating group or channel.

Automatic retries are enabled for eligible unlocked settings and require access granted by the provider. An unavailable target can add a request and delay. Strict model selections, other refusal types and work that cannot safely be repeated still stop without a retry. To disable retries, set `plugins.entries.codex.config.appServer.cyberFailover.mode` to `off` for native Codex, or `agents.defaults.embeddedAgent.cyberFailover.mode` to `off` for embedded turns. See [model failover](/concepts/model-failover) for configuration.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Show Codex policy notices and add a conditional, single-turn Daybreak retry [#143761](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143761).
- Extend eligible OpenAI cyber-policy retries to embedded turns with replay checks and preservation of completed work and interruptions [#145291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145291). Thanks @vacinc.
- Name the successful embedded retry model before the answer in the originating conversation [#147146](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147146). Thanks @vacinc.

**Bug fixes**

- Handle denied Codex fallback access with an unavailable notice and cooldown while preserving attempt results [#145105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145105).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep generated music filenames">

Generated music attachments now use the saved audio filename, including a name you supplied, so immediate results and background completion messages identify the same file.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Automations and Scheduling

Scheduled work keeps your latest edits and gives you clearer ways to follow what ran, what failed, and what needs attention.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Keep scheduled work and its outcomes current">

Changes you save to a running schedule or watcher now survive completion and crash recovery. You can search, filter, and page through run history from the CLI, with separate filters for execution and delivery results. Script automations can refresh stale plugin tools once before starting, while repeated failures stay grouped and confirmed successful completion sends a recovery notice. That setup recovery never replays a script or tool action that has already started.

Doctor now preserves scheduled reports that return to their original conversation, including setups without an external messaging channel. If an older repair already changed a job to isolated delivery, restore its intended destination manually. Disabled jobs stay disabled.

Before downgrading, let affected edited runs finish and let the scheduler finish reconciling their results on the current version. A completed task alone is not enough to preserve those edits when returning to an older version.

Administrator tasks can keep [managing existing automations](/automation/cron-jobs/managing-jobs) after waiting for subagents, without another user message. That access belongs only to the exact resumed task and covers management of existing jobs. Cancellation, a session reset or archive, a new direct user turn, or a Gateway restart ends the handoff.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Automation owners and maintenance notices">

For older automations with a recoverable creator identity, `openclaw doctor --fix` restores prompt editing without changing tool permissions. Jobs whose required creator account is unavailable now stop before execution and explain why. Restore that account or recreate the automation under the intended account; changing its delivery account does not transfer ownership. Missing execution agents also leave a reason in run history.

New update and restart notices go only to destinations matched to configured command owners, and update notices respect account send settings. Unsupported destinations lose notices and restart continuations, while update results remain in the Control UI. Review owner eligibility and account send settings if chat notices stop arriving. Later policy changes do not recall notices already queued.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Record missing-owner rejections for manual automation runs [#143804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143804). Thanks @sunnyshu0925, @obviyus, and @yncubys.
- Restore creator prompt editing for older automations [#145730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145730). Thanks @joncursi.
- Report unavailable-account and missing-agent automation failures [#145783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145783). Thanks @runvouch and @joncursi.

**Security and trust**

- Restrict lifecycle notices to configured owners [#146079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146079). Thanks @adembektas.
- Respect account send settings for update notices [#146084](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146084). Thanks @eleqtrizit and @adembektas.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Finish automatic reset hooks">

Hooks that run during daily or idle session resets can now finish delayed work after the triggering request ends. This lets a hook wait for another operation and continue its work without losing its place when that request finishes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>
