- Let delayed automatic reset hooks finish after the triggering request ends [#146350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146350). Thanks @vasubansal7576, @liuwqgit, @vincentkoc, and @yigtwxx.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Review a subagent's result before announcing it">

A parent agent can now [review a subagent's result privately](/tools/subagents/announce#private-parent-completion), ask for more work, or stay silent before anything is automatically announced to a channel. Opt in with `completionTarget: "parent"` when spawning the subagent. A busy parent handles the result in a separate private turn after its current work, and resetting or removing the parent keeps the result from reaching a replacement session.

This option supports hidden native one-shot runs. ACP, collected results, visible or threaded runs, session mode, and `expectsCompletionMessage: false` are not supported. Automatic child results, parent replies and generated media stay off the channel on this route, but permitted explicit send tools can still send messages, and operators can still inspect tool arguments and child transcripts. Leaving the option unset preserves existing behavior.

Keep a verified pre-upgrade backup and follow the target build's migration instructions, including stopping processes that write to OpenClaw's data before running `doctor --fix`. Rolling back requires the matching released build and backup, losing work saved after that backup. Older builds may discard private handoffs, so downgrading and upgrading again does not guarantee they will resume.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Return native subagent results for private parent review, follow-up work or silence before automatic channel announcements [#147206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147206). Thanks @vincentkoc, @nicknmorty, @good-warning, and @fuller-stack-dev.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Browser and Computer Use

Cloud sessions gain more ways to start from prepared work and return to it after an interruption. Desktop and browser controls also make it easier to follow what your agent is doing.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Start work from prepared cloud environments">

Private repositories can reuse eligible [prepared cloud environments](/gateway/cloud-workers/warm-images), reducing repeated setup when matching capacity is available. Preparation keeps source GitHub credentials on the computer running OpenClaw; the later connected session receives its normal credentials. Prepared repository startup also avoids extra remote calls when setting your Git author details.

Crabbox-based workers require version 0.56.0 or newer. If your host cannot download releases, stage a supported copy before updating OpenClaw, because worker inspection and cleanup need it too.

Large workspaces also receive faster file checks during preparation for node and cloud sessions. Stop can cancel those checks before a worker is allocated, with any file reads already underway finishing before cleanup. Upgrade the Gateway to receive this fix; updating only the node is not enough.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse prepared cloud environments for private repositories [#144237](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144237).
- Avoid extra remote calls when starting prepared worker repositories [#145302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145302).
- Avoid repeated checkpoint cleanup during worker startup [#145331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145331).
- Reduce GitHub round trips when starting prepared repository workers [#145480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145480).
- Defer unused syntax highlighting during cloud worker startup [#145685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145685).
- Overlap cloud worker loading with connection approval [#145793](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145793).
- Skip redundant Git author lookups during worker workspace sync [#146833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146833). Thanks @vincentkoc.

**Bug fixes**

- Reject incompatible model switches on worker-assigned conversations [#136833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/136833). Thanks @sunnyshu0925, @patrick-erichsen, @jalehman, and @skorfmann.
- Avoid loading inactive conversation branches for cloud-worker preparation [#145591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145591).
- Stop expired forks from extending snapshot demand [#146163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146163).
- Prevent large-workspace preparation stalls and honor Stop before worker allocation [#147470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147470).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Require Crabbox 0.56.0 for prepared cloud workers [#145109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145109).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Resume or recover cloud sessions">

A first message waiting for cloud setup can continue after a Gateway restart. Returning to a released session now waits for its replacement worker without reporting a false stall, and Stop prevents canceled setup from launching work later.

For a failed session, choose **Gateway · local** in **Restart session…** to [recover on the computer running OpenClaw](/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle) once worker cleanup finishes. Recovery uses the last accepted workspace checkpoint. Changes that were never uploaded may be lost, and the interrupted turn is not replayed.

Archiving an independent cloud session no longer waits behind queued maintenance for unrelated provisioning. The session stays visible as **Archiving…** until completion is confirmed, and a failure leaves an error and a chance to retry. A timeout does not mean cleanup finished.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Recover failed cloud sessions locally [#146054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146054).
- Keep a healthy Crabbox desktop renderer running on resume when its wallpaper is unchanged [#146816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146816). Thanks @vincentkoc.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep useful failure details for worker workspace transfers [#144272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144272). Thanks @jalehman.
- Resume cloud-session setup after Gateway restarts [#145068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145068).
- Show useful cloud worker recovery actions across session menus [#145153](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145153).
- Archive cloud sessions after automatically stopping startup or retrying cleanup [#145170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145170).
- Resume cloud sessions without false stalls or late worker launches after Stop [#146225](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146225).
- Avoid unrelated queued-maintenance delays and keep cloud archives visible until confirmed [#147552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147552).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Check cloud work with less repeated file processing">

Finishing cloud work can reuse hashes for unchanged files instead of repeatedly reading their contents. Large workspace scans also reuse temporary memory while hashing files. Changed files still receive the checks needed before their results are accepted.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated Git work when saving cloud-session checkpoints [#145571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145571).
- Skip empty provisioned-file archive scans [#146097](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146097).
- Reuse unchanged cloud-workspace file hashes while preserving verification [#146327](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146327).
- Reuse bounded hashing buffers during large cloud-workspace scans [#146680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146680).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Manage cloud snapshot build results">

The Snapshots page keeps build progress, cancellation controls, and failure messages visible when the image inventory fails to load. Dismissing a reviewed failure removes it and updates **Needs attention** as soon as the request succeeds. Dismissal clears the current view; the retained record can return after a page reload.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Dismiss reviewed failed cloud-worker snapshot builds from the current view [#144671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144671).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep snapshot builds visible when image inventory fails [#144127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144127).
- Remove dismissed snapshot builds immediately [#145122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145122).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Run sandboxed tools on compatible cloud workers">

The optional [Crabbox sandbox backend](/gateway/sandboxing/crabbox-backend) can run commands and file tools on a cloud machine while your agent and model credentials stay on the host. Enable and configure the plugin to use it. Initial support is direct Daytona with a compatible Crabbox build; version 0.56.0 lacks the required capability, so prepared-worker support alone is not enough. Cloud resource usage applies, and this backend does not support sandboxed browsers or Docker bind mounts.

Before downgrading to a version without this recovery support, disable the backend and finish pending cloud allocation or cleanup using the current version. The shared [SSH backend](/gateway/sandboxing/ssh-backend) also now requires native no-replace directory renaming to create or recreate workspaces. Existing workspaces remain usable without being reseeded.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Run sandboxed tools on compatible Crabbox cloud machines, preserve remote edits across reuse, and publish initial SSH workspace uploads without replacing existing work [#144454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144454).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect and control the intended desktop">

An agent running on your Gateway can now [control its configured desktop](/gateway/config-browser-ui-desktop) without a separately paired node. Explicitly enable `cua-computer`, allow the `computer` tool, and use a vision-capable agent. The configured Gateway desktop is preferred by default and reports an error if unavailable, keeping input from being redirected elsewhere. Provider setup and native display permissions still apply.

On [desktop-enabled Linux cloud workers](/gateway/cloud-sessions), agents can launch a graphical app with `exec` and operate it on the same desktop. Use `background: true` for apps that need to stay open while the agent uses them.

The viewer's **Match** option lets compatible virtual desktops resize to fit the panel when you have control and permission to resize; **Fit** remains the default. Older fixed-size workers need a backend update and reprovisioning. Supported browsers in a secure context can also open a view-only **Picture-in-Picture** window to watch the desktop beside other work. Keep the originating viewer open.

Supported [window actions](/nodes/computer-use) return a fresh screenshot and element references, so the agent can continue without a separate inspection step. After failed input or a failed refresh, get a fresh observation before deciding what to do next. Input is not automatically replayed.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Watch live desktops in a view-only Picture-in-Picture window [#143836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143836). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Match compatible virtual desktop resolution to its viewer and clear modifier keys released while native menus were open [#143938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143938). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Reduce host-selection and desktop lookup work [#146162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146162).
- Avoid repeated screenshot encoding when trimming oversized worker requests [#146907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146907). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Control the configured Gateway desktop without pairing a node [#147275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147275).

**Bug fixes**

- Honor screenshot-free accessibility inspection on updated CUA nodes; Peekaboo remains unsupported and pixel clicks still require an image [#145351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145351).
- Retain desktop input feedback and explain supported controls [#145352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145352).
- Restore desktop and portal streaming on Bun [#146005](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146005).
- Keep host availability current during event bursts [#146165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146165).
- Open graphical apps on the cloud desktop from worker commands [#146223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146223).
- Clarify supported window accessibility filters for computer-use agents [#146388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146388).
- Return fresh window images and element references after supported actions [#146822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146822). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Retain connected Desktop viewers for quick Chat tab switches within 30 seconds, pausing input while hidden and disconnecting during active drags or session changes [#147175](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147175).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect browser pages and apply policy changes">

Browser pages appear directly in the Chat panel header, leaving more room for the page, and Mac Browser panes stay visible beside menus that do not overlap them. Plain accessibility snapshots now show element references alongside their controls, so you can target follow-up actions on supported backends.

With [hot reload](/gateway/configuration/hot-reload) enabled, changes to browser enablement, JavaScript permission, and network policy no longer restart the whole Gateway. The browser service still restarts, canceling pending work and closing OpenClaw-managed Chrome tabs. Attached and remote browsers stay open, but OpenClaw disconnects from them. Extension relay settings still require a Gateway restart.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show browser pages and optional site icons in the Chat panel header [#145517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145517).
- Reload browser policy without a Gateway restart [#146100](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146100).
- Skip unnecessary HTML visibility checks [#146152](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146152).
- Format oversized browser request and error logs with less repeated work [#146375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146375).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve visible webpage text after hidden HTML [#144210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144210). Thanks @ruel225, @obviyus, and @yetval.
- Keep sidebar tab menus visible over native Browser pages [#145108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145108). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Warn when browser response-body output is shortened [#145210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145210).
- Keep Mac Browser panes visible beside nonoverlapping menus [#145482](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145482).
- Show element references in plain browser accessibility snapshots [#146247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146247).
- Report unconfirmed browser data removal when deleting a profile, rather than claiming no data exists [#146965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146965).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Save a displayed browser file">

The [Browser sidebar](/web/control-ui/panels) gains a **Download file** button for the image, video, or document you are viewing, keeping its preview open. Mac tabs use a native Save dialog. Eligible Agent tabs download through your browser; default or network-restricted Agent profiles must use **Open in your browser** instead. Remote browser-node transfers retain their 10 MiB file limit, and existing-session Chrome MCP profiles do not support this action.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Download the displayed Browser sidebar file while keeping its preview open, preserve existing files if a native save fails, and restore missing WebKit toolbar icons [#144480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144480). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Share a browser page with your agent">

A [shared Browser dashboard](/web/dashboards#share-a-browser-dashboard-with-your-agent) lets you and your agent use the same page. Enter a note or change an app's filters, then ask your agent to continue from what you changed. It uses a local OpenClaw-managed browser profile and that profile's login state, including for permitted HTTP apps shown through the HTTPS Control UI.

Browser access is required. Remote profiles, paired-node browsers, and attached personal browsers are unsupported, and the page does not use your phone or laptop's cookies. **Stop** closes the tab and loses unsaved page state. **Resume** opens the saved URL.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Share clicks, typing, and navigation with your agent in one managed-browser dashboard, with saved Stop and Resume controls [#146702](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146702).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Choose which machine handles browser actions">

Automatic browser actions now prefer the selected browser on the computer running your Gateway, including an installed managed browser that has not started yet. When that computer has no browser capability, a single eligible connected node can handle the request. Explicit targets and configured node pins keep their precedence, and failures stay with the selected browser instead of replaying the action on another machine.

This default can change which machine and browser login session you use after upgrading. To keep using a node's existing session, [select that node explicitly or set `gateway.nodes.browser.node`](/tools/browser/remote).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Prefer the Gateway host's selected browser for automatic tool and CLI requests, while preserving explicit node selection [#147446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147446).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use real paths for browser output">

Browser downloads and traces preserve the directory name you requested, including trailing spaces, and check the whole path before writing. If your output directory passes through a user-created symbolic link, [choose its real, canonical path](/tools/browser/troubleshooting). The **Invalid path** error can now apply even when the final directory already exists. The macOS `/tmp` and `/var` system aliases remain supported.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve literal browser output directory names, reject symlinked ancestors, and clean up temporary Chrome MCP screenshot workspaces [#147541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147541).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Plugins and Integrations

You can add supported plugins and keep working in the same conversation. There are also more ways to share selected conversations, carry agent settings between installations, and work with the history your integrations save.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Install plugins and continue the conversation">

Owners can [install and reload supported plugins](/plugins/manage-plugins) from the CLI or an authorized chat command without restarting a running Gateway. The Plugins UI offers official and ClawHub packages, with existing permission checks and consent still in place. Explicitly approved package activation also works when passive reload is disabled. Managed Codex conversations can pick up changed tools while carrying completed results and accepted follow-up input forward; that handoff stops the current native turn and background terminals. Accepted chats still waiting to start can wait through plugin reload and continue after replacement or successful rollback without being resent.

Local paths, archives, local Git repositories and marketplace registrations must be available on the Gateway computer. Offline installs wait for startup. If an installation saves but activation fails, follow its repair message and run `openclaw plugins reload`. An update or repair that loses permission to continue stops and retains installed files and records for a fresh operation. Imported or supervised Codex sessions need a new managed conversation for changed tools. Enabling experimental native plugin UI still requires a Gateway restart and browser reload.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Retain plugin dependencies through active calls and cleanup [#144252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144252).
- Preserve original plugin registration objects and handlers [#145488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145488). Thanks @romneyda.
- Keep plugin callbacks working when another instance retires [#145804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145804).
- Recover plugin reloads and retain cleanup for late-starting services [#145816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145816).
- Add native compiled-plugin loading on Bun, with a remaining Bun 1.4.0 dependency-resolution risk [#145947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145947).
- Release retired service state after plugin reloads [#146073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146073). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Release completed plugin reload state in long-running Gateways [#146239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146239).
- Apply approved plugin packages through the running Gateway and give accurate reload guidance [#146413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146413).
- Apply first channel setup through live plugin reload [#146481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146481).
- Preserve the original plugin update error through rollback failures [#146483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146483). Thanks @aniruddhaadak80.
- Reuse completed plugin registrations for prepared-metadata loads [#146703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146703).
- Let accepted chats waiting to start continue after plugin replacement or successful rollback [#147472](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147472).
- Preserve plugin files and records when update or repair ownership is lost, support saved-secret model setup, and guide recovery after a selected sign-in is removed [#144316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144316). Thanks @vincentkoc, @fuller-stack-dev.

**Improvements**

- Install and reload supported plugins without restarting the Gateway [#145484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145484).

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Find plugins by purpose and inspect them">

Purpose-based categories and package logos make plugins easier to recognize and browse. Marketplace pages keep their destination when you refresh, and a selected detail tab stays open as information arrives. Plugin authors also get clearer build and verification errors when a package is missing its manifest or a declared JavaScript entry. Builds preserve existing manifest and package-folder access permissions.
